"""Candidate lossless NPZ serialization implementation; installation is external.

Retains field order, dtype, shape and every array byte. Atomic rename, producer
SHA publication, paired retention, failure preservation, disk guards and lifecycle
remain the caller's unchanged responsibilities. There is no asynchronous writer.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from typing import BinaryIO, Mapping, Sequence
import zipfile

import numpy as np


def _keys(arrays: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> list[str]:
    keys = list(arrays)
    if not keys or any(not isinstance(k, str) or not k or '/' in k or '\\' in k for k in keys):
        raise ValueError('Expected nonempty simple NPZ field names')
    if any(np.asarray(arrays[k]).dtype.hasobject for k in keys):
        raise ValueError('Object arrays are outside the actual trace schema')
    return keys


def write_arrays_npz(file: BinaryIO, arrays: Mapping[str, np.ndarray], *, compresslevel: int) -> None:
    """Public NPY/ZIP APIs, explicit DEFLATE level; no dtype conversion."""
    keys = _keys(arrays)
    if compresslevel not in range(1, 10):
        raise ValueError('Use an explicit DEFLATE level between 1 and 9')
    with zipfile.ZipFile(file, mode='w', compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED,
                         compresslevel=compresslevel, allowZip64=True) as archive:
        for key in keys:
            with archive.open(key + '.npy', mode='w', force_zip64=True) as member:
                np.lib.format.write_array(member, np.asanyarray(arrays[key]), allow_pickle=False)


def write_trace_rows_npz(file: BinaryIO, rows: Sequence[Mapping[str, np.ndarray]], *, compresslevel: int) -> None:
    """Stack one field at a time; keep original rows alive until caller commits."""
    if not rows:
        raise ValueError('No trace rows')
    keys = _keys(rows[0])
    for row in rows:
        if list(row) != keys:
            raise ValueError('Trace row field order/schema mismatch')
        _keys(row)
    if compresslevel not in range(1, 10):
        raise ValueError('Use an explicit DEFLATE level between 1 and 9')
    with zipfile.ZipFile(file, mode='w', compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED,
                         compresslevel=compresslevel, allowZip64=True) as archive:
        for key in keys:
            value = np.stack([row[key] for row in rows])
            with archive.open(key + '.npy', mode='w', force_zip64=True) as member:
                np.lib.format.write_array(member, value, allow_pickle=False)
            del value


def sha256_file(path: str | Path, *, block_bytes: int = 8 * 1024**2) -> str:
    """Same final file SHA, bounded read buffer; retains the full read pass."""
    if block_bytes <= 0:
        raise ValueError('block_bytes must be positive')
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open('rb') as file:
        for block in iter(lambda: file.read(block_bytes), b''):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()

# Copyright (c) 2026 SCUTRobotLab, SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT.
# Original ballistic reference body copied without AST changes from fixed commit
# b8ff79f3df855faf9dc92f4a282bd80c42649466. MOD4 wrapper and target mapping
# are separate below. CPU candidate only; no force/torque assistance or air reset.
import torch
from types import SimpleNamespace
def _get_ballistic_reference(self, env, cfg: dict) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    traj_cfg = cfg.get('trajectory', {})
    gravity = max(float(traj_cfg.get('gravity', 9.81)), 1e-06)
    push_start_height = float(traj_cfg.get('push_start_height', 0.2))
    release_height = float(traj_cfg.get('release_height', 0.38))
    peak_height = env.jump_takeoff_ref_target_height
    timing_mode = traj_cfg.get('tuck_timing_mode', 'dynamic_push_time')
    if timing_mode == 'fixed_tuck_time':
        push_time = torch.full_like(peak_height, max(float(traj_cfg.get('fixed_tuck_time_s', 0.14)), env.step_dt))
        discriminant = torch.square(gravity * push_time) + 8.0 * gravity * torch.clamp(peak_height - push_start_height, min=0.0)
        release_vel_z = 0.5 * (-gravity * push_time + torch.sqrt(discriminant))
        raw_release_height = push_start_height + 0.5 * release_vel_z * push_time
        max_release_height = float(traj_cfg.get('max_release_height', release_height))
        release_height_for_flight = torch.clamp(raw_release_height, max=max_release_height)
        release_vel_z = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(2.0 * gravity * (peak_height - release_height_for_flight), min=0.0))
    else:
        flight_peak_time = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(2.0 * (peak_height - release_height) / gravity, min=0.0))
        release_vel_z = gravity * flight_peak_time
        push_distance = max(release_height - push_start_height, 0.0)
        push_time = torch.where(release_vel_z > 1e-06, 2.0 * push_distance / release_vel_z, torch.zeros_like(release_vel_z))
        release_height_for_flight = torch.full_like(peak_height, release_height)
    push_accel = torch.where(push_time > 1e-06, release_vel_z / push_time, torch.zeros_like(release_vel_z))
    elapsed = torch.clamp(env.jump_takeoff_phase_time, min=0.0)
    push_elapsed = torch.minimum(elapsed, torch.clamp(push_time, min=0.0))
    push_height = push_start_height + 0.5 * push_accel * torch.square(push_elapsed)
    push_vel_z = push_accel * push_elapsed
    flight_elapsed = torch.clamp(elapsed - push_time, min=0.0)
    flight_height = release_height_for_flight + release_vel_z * flight_elapsed - 0.5 * gravity * torch.square(flight_elapsed)
    flight_vel_z = release_vel_z - gravity * flight_elapsed
    ref_height = torch.where(elapsed <= push_time, push_height, flight_height)
    ref_vel_z = torch.where(elapsed <= push_time, push_vel_z, flight_vel_z)
    return (ref_height, ref_vel_z, push_time)

def reference(*,elapsed,peak_height,push_start_height,release_height,control_dt=.01,
              timing_mode='dynamic_push_time',fixed_tuck_time_s=.14):
    if not all(torch.isfinite(v).all() for v in (elapsed,peak_height)):
        raise ValueError('nonfinite trajectory input')
    if not (peak_height>release_height).all() or not release_height>push_start_height:
        raise ValueError('Require peak>actual release>actual push-start height')
    if timing_mode not in ('dynamic_push_time','fixed_tuck_time') or control_dt!=.01:
        raise ValueError('Unqualified H1 timing mode or 100Hz control clock')
    env=SimpleNamespace(jump_takeoff_ref_target_height=peak_height,
                        jump_takeoff_phase_time=elapsed,step_dt=control_dt)
    cfg={'trajectory':dict(gravity=9.81,push_start_height=push_start_height,
         release_height=release_height,tuck_timing_mode=timing_mode,
         fixed_tuck_time_s=fixed_tuck_time_s,max_release_height=release_height)}
    return _get_ballistic_reference(None,env,cfg)

def peak_body_height_from_actual_wheel_geometry(*,body_world_z,wheel_bottom_world_z,
                                               reference_ground_world_z,
                                               platform_top_world_z,margin_m):
    if not all(torch.isfinite(v).all() for v in (body_world_z,wheel_bottom_world_z,
             reference_ground_world_z,platform_top_world_z)):
        raise ValueError('nonfinite actual geometry')
    if margin_m<0 or not ((platform_top_world_z-reference_ground_world_z)>=.270).all():
        raise ValueError('Platform below the human 270mm minimum')
    offset=(body_world_z-wheel_bottom_world_z.amin(-1)).clamp_min(0)
    return platform_top_world_z-reference_ground_world_z+offset+margin_m

"""Source-only72 authority adapter; never creates/copies/initializes a ledger."""
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json
import math
import re

STATE=Path('/home/lc01/Zonglin Li/zonglin2/state/mod4_single_canonical_authority_20261026')
CANONICAL=STATE/'TRAINING_BUDGET_10H.json'
LOCKS=(STATE/'native_floor_supervisor.lock',STATE/'autonomy_controller.lock')
AUTHORIZATION=STATE/'HUMAN_TEMPORARY_UNLIMITED_AUTHORIZATION_ACTUAL.json'
HANDOFF=STATE/'AUTHORITY_HANDOFF_ACTUAL.json'
GENERATION='MOD4_SINGLE_WRITER72_20261026'
MODE='temporary_unlimited_until_single4090_returns'
FINITE_MODE='finite_original_limits_restored_after_single4090_return'

def validate_authority_handoff(handoff,authorization_SHA):
    assert authorization_SHA==AUTH_SHA and handoff['authorization_file_SHA256']==AUTH_SHA
    assert handoff['schema']=='mod4.single_writer72.authority_handoff.v1'
    assert handoff['authority_generation']==GENERATION and handoff['authority_host']=='72'
    assert handoff['activated'] is True and handoff['approved'] is True and handoff['old_authority_retired'] is True
    assert handoff['canonical_path']==str(CANONICAL)
    assert handoff['owner_locks']==[str(path) for path in LOCKS]
    assert handoff['canonical_copied'] is False and handoff['historical_records_reconstructed'] is False
    assert handoff['old_canonical_SHA256']==OLD_SHA
    assert handoff['known_last_settled_run_id']=='THREE4090_EXPERT_ORIGINAL_1792994651814636100'
    assert handoff['latest_original_owner_allout'] is True and handoff['latest_original_active_segment'] is None
    assert re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}',handoff['seed_canonical_SHA256'])
    assert handoff['budget_mode']==MODE
    return True


def read_authority():
    raw=AUTHORIZATION.read_bytes();handoff=json.loads(HANDOFF.read_bytes())
    validate_authority_handoff(handoff,hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest())
    return handoff


def validate_tracking_schema(c,handoff):
    assert c['authority_generation']==GENERATION
    assert c.get('budget_mode',c.get('mode')) in (MODE,FINITE_MODE)
    if c.get('budget_mode',c.get('mode'))==MODE:assert c['active_spending_cap_seconds'] is None
    assert c['authorized_training_seconds']==2268000
    assert c['historical_budget_extension_count']==9 and c['new_authority_budget_extensions']==[]
    assert c.get('budget_extensions',[])==[], 'Do not synthesize nine old extension objects'
    assert c['historical_charged_training_seconds']==HISTORICAL_CHARGED
    assert isinstance(c['completed_segments'],list)
    for key in ('charged_training_seconds','remaining_training_seconds'):
        assert isinstance(c[key],(int,float)) and not isinstance(c[key],bool) and math.isfinite(c[key])
    assert c['charged_training_seconds']>=HISTORICAL_CHARGED-1e-5
    assert abs(c['charged_training_seconds']+c['remaining_training_seconds']-2268000)<1e-5
    # Negative tracking remainder is truthful tracking, not an unlimited-mode GO limit.
    assert handoff['authority_generation']==c['authority_generation']
    return True


def authority_tracking_schema_admitted(c):
    return validate_tracking_schema(c,read_authority())


def authority_original_extension_count(c):
    authority_tracking_schema_admitted(c)
    return c['historical_budget_extension_count']+len(c['new_authority_budget_extensions'])


def authority_budget_GO_allowed(c):
    handoff=read_authority();validate_tracking_schema(c,handoff)
    if c.get('budget_mode',c.get('mode'))==FINITE_MODE:
        receipt=json.loads(RESTORATION.read_bytes())
        assert receipt['approved'] is True and receipt['limits_restored'] is True
        assert receipt['authority_generation']==GENERATION and receipt['single4090_returned'] is True
        assert receipt['budget_mode']==FINITE_MODE and receipt['minimum_remaining_seconds']==86412.86069250107
        return c['remaining_training_seconds']>86412.86069250107
    assert not RESTORATION.exists(), 'Finite restoration receipt blocks stale unlimited GO'
    assert handoff.get('single4090_returned',False) is False and handoff.get('temporary_unlimited_ended',False) is False
    # Explicit human mode overrides only the numeric spending cap. No Infinity,
    # fake extension, refund, cost clamp, second writer, or old210 fallback.
    return True

===== FULL UTF8 ENTRY C:\Users\lulu\Desktop\claude\mjcf\MOD4_H1_SCUT_STEP270_ON_210_E3_CONTINUATION_20261021\MOD4_H1_FUDAN270_QUALIFIED_JUMP_PROCESS_RESOURCE_V123_20261029\payloads_v2\seed28\source\actual_DR_memory_getter_v1.py SEGMENT 0 =====
"""Read original D0-D7 component memory only, at initial/selected reset boundaries.

No physics setter, backend material getter, random draw or simulator import.
Memory recipe values are not an independent actual PhysX readback qualification.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
import torch

FIELD_NAMES=('material_static_friction','material_dynamic_friction','material_restitution',
 'body_mass','body_inertia','body_com','joint_armature','joint_friction',
 'joint_stiffness','joint_damping','motor_episode_scale','gas_preload_target',
 'action_lag_steps','leg_encoder_episode_bias')

def require(value,message):
    if not value:raise ValueError(message)

def tensor(value):
    if torch.is_tensor(value):return value
    if hasattr(value,'torch') and torch.is_tensor(value.torch):return value.torch
    raise ValueError('Expected existing torch tensor or SDK ProxyArray.torch; no implicit backend conversion')

def finite(value,shape,name,device=None,dtype=torch.float32):
    value=tensor(value)
    require(tuple(value.shape)==tuple(shape),name+': shape mismatch')
    require(value.dtype==dtype,name+': dtype mismatch')
    require(device is None or value.device==torch.device(device),name+': device mismatch')
    require(bool(torch.isfinite(value).all()),name+': nonfinite')
    return value

def indices(value,total):
    if isinstance(value,slice):return list(range(total))[value]
    if hasattr(value,'torch'):value=value.torch
    if torch.is_tensor(value):
        return value.detach().cpu().tolist() # only initial/reset order check, never per-control
    return list(value)

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RuntimeOrderBinding:
    body_names: tuple
    joint_names: tuple
    leg_joint_names: tuple
    wheel_joint_names: tuple
    gas_joint_names: tuple
    material_shape_count: int

def read_old_processed_applied(env,issue_tick):
    """Before submit at a clean control boundary. Never raw policy action6."""
    require(isinstance(issue_tick,int) and not isinstance(issue_tick,bool) and issue_tick>=0 and issue_tick%4==0,'control boundary issue tick required')
    q=env._physics_action_delay.queue;n=env.num_envs;d=env.device
    require(q.n==n and q.physics_us==2500 and q.control_us==10000,'original queue clock/scope required')
    result=[]
    for key,width in (('legs',4),('wheels',2)):
        g=q.groups[key]
        finite(g['last_apply_tick'],(n,),key+'.last_apply_tick',d,torch.int64)
        finite(g['pending_valid'],(n,),key+'.pending_valid',d,torch.bool)
        require(bool((g['last_apply_tick']==issue_tick-1).all()),'queue application clock mismatch')
        require(not bool(g['pending_valid'].any()),'pending target cannot be discarded')
        result.append(finite(g['applied'],(n,width),key+'.applied',d).clone())
    return torch.cat(result,-1)

class ActualDRMemoryGetter:
    def __init__(self,env,binding:RuntimeOrderBinding):
        self.env=env;self.binding=binding;self.n=env.num_envs;self.device=torch.device(env.device)
        self.robot=env.scene['robot'];self.cache=None;self.counts=None;self.friction_transfer_rows=0
        self._check_order()

    def _check_order(self):
        r=self.robot;b=self.binding;j=len(b.joint_names)
        require(tuple(r.body_names)==b.body_names and tuple(r.joint_names)==b.joint_names,'actual body/joint order differs from pinned binding')
        require(len(set(b.body_names))==len(b.body_names) and len(set(b.joint_names))==j and j==30,'unique ordered30 joints required')
        require(b.body_names.count('chassis')==1 and b.material_shape_count>0,'chassis/material binding required')
        for key,expected,width in (('legs',b.leg_joint_names,4),('wheels',b.wheel_joint_names,2),('gas_springs',b.gas_joint_names,2)):
            ids=indices(r.actuators[key].joint_indices,j)
            require(len(ids)==width and tuple(b.joint_names[i] for i in ids)==expected,'actuator order mismatch '+key)
        require(tuple(self.env.cfg.events.gas_spring_preload.params['joint_names'])==b.gas_joint_names,'gas preload order mismatch')
        require(self.env._chassis_com.body_id==b.body_names.index('chassis'),'COM body id mismatch')
        require(tuple(self.env._spring_pressure.names)==b.gas_joint_names,'D5 gas names mismatch')
        gas_ids=[b.joint_names.index(x) for x in b.gas_joint_names]
        require(list(self.env._spring_pressure.ids)==list(self.env._gas_damping.ids)==gas_ids,'D5/D6 gas joint order mismatch')
        require(self.env._explicit_pd.leg_ids==[b.joint_names.index(x) for x in b.leg_joint_names],'D7 leg order mismatch')
        require(self.env._explicit_pd.wheel_ids==[b.joint_names.index(x) for x in b.wheel_joint_names],'D7 wheel order mismatch')

    def _ids(self,ids):
        ids=torch.as_tensor(ids,device=self.device)
        require(ids.dtype==torch.int64 and ids.ndim==1 and len(ids.unique())==len(ids),'unique int64 reset ids required')
        require(bool(((ids>=0)&(ids<self.n)).all()),'reset ids outside population')
        return ids

    def _counts(self):
        e=self.env;q=e._physics_action_delay.queue
        values=dict(D0=e._mild_dr.reset_count,D1=e._sensor_mild.episode,D5=e._spring_pressure.component.episode,
                    D6=e._gas_damping.component.episode,D7COM=e._chassis_com.component.episode,
                    D7PD=e._explicit_pd.component.episode,D4legs=q.groups['legs']['episode'],D4wheels=q.groups['wheels']['episode'])
        return {k:finite(v,(self.n,),k,self.device,torch.int64).clone() for k,v in values.items()}

    def _read(self,ids):
        self._check_order();e=self.env;r=self.robot;b=self.binding;n=self.n;d=self.device
        nb=len(b.body_names);nj=len(b.joint_names);m=b.material_shape_count;dr=e._mild_dr
        factor=finite(dr.factor,(n,),'D0.factor',d)
        require(bool((factor>0).all()),'mass factor must be positive')
        mass=finite(dr.mass,(n,nb),'D0.nominal_mass',d)[ids]*factor[ids,None]
        inertia=finite(dr.inertia,(n,nb,9),'D0.nominal_inertia',d)[ids]*factor[ids,None,None]
        original_com=finite(e._chassis_com.original_pose,(n,nb,7),'COM.original_pose',d)
        delta=finite(e._chassis_com.component.delta_m,(n,3),'COM.delta_m',d)
        com=original_com[ids,:,:3].clone();com[:,e._chassis_com.body_id]+=delta[ids]
        # D0 already caches baseline full materials and current per-env mu on CPU.
        # Only selected reset rows cross to the model device; no get_material_properties.
        materials=finite(dr.materials,(n,m,3),'D0.materials_CPU','cpu')
        mu=finite(dr.mu,(n,),'D0.mu_CPU','cpu')
        cpu_ids=ids.to('cpu');mat=materials[cpu_ids].clone()
        wheel=list(dr.wheel_shapes)
        require(len(wheel)>=2 and len(set(wheel))==len(wheel) and all(0<=x<m for x in wheel),'wheel material shape indices invalid')
        mat[:,wheel,0]=mu[cpu_ids,None];mat[:,wheel,1]=mu[cpu_ids,None]
        mat[:,wheel,2]=0. # EXACT original mild_dr.__call__ wheel restitution write, not a missing-field default.
        mat=mat.to(d);self.friction_transfer_rows+=len(ids)
        # SDK data buffers are direct existing parameter memory, not root_view reads.
        arm=finite(r.data.joint_armature,(n,nj),'joint_armature',d)[ids].clone()
        friction=finite(r.data.joint_friction_coeff,(n,nj),'joint_static_friction',d)[ids].clone()
        stiffness=finite(r.data.joint_stiffness,(n,nj),'implicit_stiffness',d)[ids].clone()
        damping=finite(r.data.joint_damping,(n,nj),'implicit_damping',d)[ids].clone()
        # Explicit motor coefficients are NOT the zero implicit drive buffers.
        pd=e._explicit_pd;coeff=pd.component.coefficients()
        for key,field,value,width in (('legs','stiffness','leg_kp',4),('legs','damping','leg_kd',4),('wheels','damping','wheel_kd',2)):
            actual=finite(getattr(r.actuators[key],field),(n,width),key+'.'+field,d)
            expected=finite(coeff[value],(n,width),'PD.'+value,d)
            require(torch.equal(actual[ids],expected[ids]),'D7 component/model disagreement '+value)
        for key,which in (('legs',pd.leg_ids),('wheels',pd.wheel_ids)):
            stiffness[:,which]=finite(r.actuators[key].stiffness,(n,len(which)),key+'.stiffness',d)[ids]
            damping[:,which]=finite(r.actuators[key].damping,(n,len(which)),key+'.damping',d)[ids]
        gas=e._spring_pressure;gasd=e._gas_damping
        gk=finite(gas.component.actual_stiffness(gas.nominal),(n,2),'D5.actual_stiffness',d)
        gd=finite(gasd.component.actual_damping_Ns_per_m(),(n,2),'D6.actual_damping',d)
        require(torch.equal(stiffness[:,gas.ids],gk[ids]),'D5 buffer/component disagreement')
        require(torch.equal(damping[:,gas.ids],gd[ids]),'D6 buffer/component disagreement')
        strengths=[]
        for key,width in (('legs',4),('wheels',2)):
            a=r.actuators[key]
            strengths.append(finite(a.episode_scale,(n,width),key+'.episode_scale',d)[ids])
            # This pinned mild profile promises exactly no per-step gain noise.
            step=finite(a.step_scale,(n,width),key+'.step_scale',d)
            require(bool((step[ids]==1).all()),'active dynamic motor step_scale is outside this static getter contract')
        preload=torch.as_tensor(e.cfg.events.gas_spring_preload.params['targets'],device=d,dtype=torch.float32)
        finite(preload,(2,),'gas_preload_config')
        q=e._physics_action_delay.queue
        require(q.physics_us==2500 and q.control_us==10000 and q.n==n,'D4 clock/scope mismatch')
        lag=torch.stack([finite(q.groups[k]['lag_steps'],(n,),'lag '+k,d,torch.int64)[ids] for k in ('legs','wheels')],-1)
        require(bool(((lag>=0)&(lag<=2)).all()),'D4 lag invalid')
        result=dict(material_static_friction=mat[:,:,0],material_dynamic_friction=mat[:,:,1],material_restitution=mat[:,:,2],
            body_mass=mass,body_inertia=inertia,body_com=com,joint_armature=arm,joint_friction=friction,
            joint_stiffness=stiffness,joint_damping=damping,motor_episode_scale=torch.cat(strengths,-1),
            gas_preload_target=preload[None,:].expand(len(ids),-1).clone(),action_lag_steps=lag.float(),
            leg_encoder_episode_bias=finite(e._sensor_mild.bias,(n,4),'sensor.episode_bias',d)[ids].clone())
        require(set(result)==set(FIELD_NAMES),'14 field catalog mismatch')
        for k,v in result.items():require(bool(torch.isfinite(v).all()),k+': nonfinite output')
        return {k:v.detach().clone() for k,v in result.items()}

    def capture_initial(self):
        require(self.cache is None,'initial snapshot already captured')
        values=self._read(torch.arange(self.n,device=self.device));counts=self._counts()
        self.cache=values;self.counts=counts
        return self.snapshot()

    def reset_ids_since_capture(self):
        require(self.cache is not None,'initial capture required')
        current=self._counts();difference=current['D0']-self.counts['D0']
        require(bool(((difference==0)|(difference==1)).all()),'missed/reversed/multiple D0 reset epochs')
        for k in current:require(torch.equal(current[k]-self.counts[k],difference),'reset hooks incomplete or inconsistent: '+k)
        return torch.where(difference==1)[0]

    def refresh_after_resets(self,ids=None):
        detected=self.reset_ids_since_capture()
        if ids is not None:
            ids=self._ids(ids)
            require(torch.equal(ids.sort().values,detected),'declared reset ids differ from component epochs')
        if not len(detected):return dict(updated=0,friction_transfer_rows=0,nonreset_xi_cached_exact=True)
        values=self._read(detected);counts=self._counts()
        # Atomic publication: all selected values validated before cache changes.
        updated={k:v.clone() for k,v in self.cache.items()}
        for k in updated:updated[k][detected]=values[k]
        self.cache=updated;self.counts=counts
        return dict(updated=len(detected),friction_transfer_rows=len(detected),nonreset_xi_cached_exact=True)

    def snapshot(self):
        require(self.cache is not None,'initial capture required')
        return {k:v.clone() for k,v in self.cache.items()}

    def xi(self):
        require(self.cache is not None,'initial capture required')
        return torch.cat([self.cache[k].flatten(1) for k in FIELD_NAMES],-1)

    def field_shapes(self):
        return {k:tuple(v.shape[1:]) for k,v in self.snapshot().items()}

"""Lossless-to-randomized-parameters static xi31 for the pinned MOD4 recipe.

Full typed DR snapshots remain admission/save evidence. This projection retains
all episode-varying degrees of freedom; nominal constants/order are source-pinned.
It never reads the robot, changes parameters or synthesizes missing fields.
"""
import torch
FIELDS=(('mass_inertia_factor',1),('wheel_friction',1),('motor_gain6',6),
        ('chassis_COM_delta3',3),('PD_factor10',10),('spring_factor2',2),
        ('gas_damping_factor2',2),('action_lag_steps2',2),('leg_encoder_bias4',4))
DIM=sum(n for _,n in FIELDS)

def compact_static_snapshot(snapshot, *, nominal_mass, nominal_inertia,
    original_body_com, body_id, leg_ids, wheel_ids, gas_ids,
    wheel_shape_ids, nominal_gas_stiffness):
    v=snapshot
    n=v['body_mass'].shape[0]
    d=v['body_mass'].device
    def require(ok,msg):
        if not bool(ok):raise ValueError(msg)
    def positive(x):
        require(torch.isfinite(x).all() and (x>0).all(),'positive finite nominal parameter required')
    mass=nominal_mass
    positive(mass)
    factor=v['body_mass'][:,body_id:body_id+1]/mass[:,body_id:body_id+1]
    # These exact recipe reconstruction tests are engineering float32 comparisons,
    # not a new physical/skill gate or sub-resolution world-point audit.
    require(torch.allclose(v['body_mass'],mass*factor,rtol=2e-6,atol=1e-7),'nonuniform mass DR not represented by factor1')
    require(torch.allclose(v['body_inertia'],nominal_inertia*factor[...,None],rtol=2e-6,atol=1e-8),'inertia DR not represented by factor1')
    mu=v['material_static_friction'][:,wheel_shape_ids[:1]]
    for name in ['material_static_friction','material_dynamic_friction']:
        require(torch.equal(v[name][:,wheel_shape_ids],mu.expand(n,len(wheel_shape_ids))),'wheel friction not shared scalar')
    require(torch.equal(v['material_restitution'][:,wheel_shape_ids],torch.zeros_like(v['material_restitution'][:,wheel_shape_ids])),'wheel restitution outside pinned recipe')
    com_delta=v['body_com'][:,body_id]-original_body_com[:,body_id]
    other=[i for i in range(v['body_com'].shape[1]) if i!=body_id]
    require(torch.equal(v['body_com'][:,other],original_body_com[:,other]),'nonchassis COM variation not represented')
    pd=torch.cat((v['joint_stiffness'][:,leg_ids]/60.,
                  v['joint_damping'][:,leg_ids]/2.,
                  v['joint_damping'][:,wheel_ids]/.6),-1)
    positive(nominal_gas_stiffness)
    spring=v['joint_stiffness'][:,gas_ids]/nominal_gas_stiffness
    gas=v['joint_damping'][:,gas_ids]/3.
    values=(factor,mu,v['motor_episode_scale'],com_delta,pd,spring,gas,
            v['action_lag_steps'],v['leg_encoder_episode_bias'])
    for (name,width),x in zip(FIELDS,values):
        require(x.shape==(n,width) and x.device==d and x.dtype==torch.float32 and torch.isfinite(x).all(),name+' wrong shape/device/type/nonfinite')
    out=torch.cat(values,-1).detach().clone()
    require(out.shape==(n,31),'explicit xi31 required')
    return out

def normalize_static31(x):
    if x.shape[-1]!=31:raise ValueError('explicit MOD4 static xi31 required')
    # Fixed feature scales, no future dynamic labels or per-step parameter query.
    center=x.new_tensor([1.,.9]+[1.]*6+[0.]*3+[1.]*10+[1.]*2+[1.]*2+[1.]*2+[0.]*4)
    scale=x.new_tensor([.1,.3]+[.1]*6+[.005]*3+[.05]*10+[.1]*2+[.1]*2+[1.]*2+[.02]*4)
    return (x-center)/scale

"""MOD4-owned converted jump goal and native SuperMimic analytic heads.

Reference values and masks share the same table for real labels, policy goals and
imagined returns. The real plant, original hard termination, V8 trace and success
callbacks remain separate. No motion file is itself a successful physical case.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
import torch
from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.geometry import invariant_features, quat_to_mat
from compact_episode_static31_v1 import normalize_static31
from static_dynamic_delay_contract_v1 import task_relative_observation

def require(ok,message):
    if not bool(ok):raise ValueError(message)

class ConvertedMOD4Reference:
    def __init__(self,document,device='cpu',*,order_scope='unbound'):
        require(document.get('schema')=='mod4.own_step_converted_reference.v1','explicit converted MOD4 reference schema required; old partial standing jump not a step reference')
        self.document=document;self.device=torch.device(device);self.order_scope=order_scope
        self.names=tuple(document['joint_names'])
        require(len(self.names)==30 and len(set(self.names))==30,'30 unique source joint names required')
        self.ages=torch.tensor(document['ages'],device=device,dtype=torch.long)
        require(self.ages.ndim==1 and len(self.ages)>=2 and self.ages[0]==0,'reference must include fresh age0')
        require(torch.equal(self.ages,torch.arange(len(self.ages),device=device)),'own reference needs each original control age; no hidden clock stretching')
        self.values=torch.tensor(document['packed73'],device=device,dtype=torch.float32)
        mask=torch.tensor(document['mask73'],device=device,dtype=torch.float32)
        if mask.ndim==1:mask=mask[None,:].expand(len(self.ages),-1).clone()
        self.mask=mask
        require(self.values.shape==(len(self.ages),73) and self.mask.shape==self.values.shape,'explicit reference/mask73 shape required')
        require(torch.isfinite(self.values).all() and torch.isfinite(mask).all(),'reference finite required')
        require(((mask==0)|(mask==1)).all(),'explicit binary known/unknown reference masks required')
        require((mask[:,3:7]==1).all(),'orientation reference required')
        require(torch.allclose(self.values[:,3:7].norm(dim=-1),torch.ones(len(self.ages),device=device),rtol=1e-5,atol=1e-5),'wxyz quaternion norm mismatch')
        self.metadata=document['metadata']
        require(self.metadata['control_dt_s']==.01,'original100Hz control clock required')
        require(self.metadata.get('foreign_transitions_for_world_model') is False,'foreign dynamics are not MOD4 WM truth')
        require(self.metadata.get('own_step_edge_x_m')==.35 and self.metadata.get('own_platform_height_m')==.27,'original270mm own terrain required')
        require(self.metadata.get('physical_success_unchanged') is True,'original true physical success criteria required')
        require(self.metadata.get('endpoint_policy')=='hold_pose_zero_velocities','explicit post-table endpoint policy required')
        self.leg_ids=[self.names.index(n) for n in self.metadata['actuator_leg_joint_names']]
        self.joint_scale=torch.tensor(self.metadata['joint_position_range_scale30'],device=device,dtype=torch.float32)
        require(self.joint_scale.shape==(30,) and (self.joint_scale>0).all() and torch.isfinite(self.joint_scale).all(),'own joint range scale required')

    def bind_named_order(self,actual_joint_names,*,scope):
        require(scope in ('source_CPU_fixture','actual_SDK_readback'),'explicit source-vs-SDK mapping scope required')
        actual=tuple(actual_joint_names)
        require(len(actual)==30 and set(actual)==set(self.names),'actual joint name set differs')
        permutation=[self.names.index(n) for n in actual]
        cols=[*range(13),*[13+i for i in permutation],*[43+i for i in permutation]]
        copied=dict(self.document)
        copied['joint_names']=list(actual)
        copied['packed73']=self.values[:,cols].cpu().tolist()
        copied['mask73']=self.mask[:,cols].cpu().tolist()
        copied['metadata']=dict(self.metadata,joint_position_range_scale30=self.joint_scale[permutation].cpu().tolist())
        # Scope records caller readback, not a substitute for signed actual SDK admission.
        return ConvertedMOD4Reference(copied,self.device,order_scope=scope)

    def to(self,device):
        return ConvertedMOD4Reference(self.document,device,order_scope=self.order_scope)

    def query(self,frames):
        idx=frames.long().clamp(0,len(self.ages)-1)
        value=self.values[idx].clone()
        beyond=frames.long()>self.ages[-1]
        value[...,7:13]=torch.where(beyond[...,None],torch.zeros_like(value[...,7:13]),value[...,7:13])
        value[...,43:73]=torch.where(beyond[...,None],torch.zeros_like(value[...,43:73]),value[...,43:73])
        return value,self.mask[idx]

    def future_goal(self,frames,num_frames):
        require(num_frames>=1,'positive future horizon required')
        result=[]
        for offset in range(num_frames):
            value,mask=self.query(frames+offset)
            result.append(torch.cat((value*mask,mask),-1))
        return torch.cat(result,-1)

class ConvertedMOD4JumpMdp:
    num_joints=30;action_dim=6;auxiliary_dim=54;packed_state_dim=127
    context_dim=31;obs_dim=86;goal_frame_dim=146
    step_edge_x_m=.35;platform_top_z_m=.27
    max_step_reward=.06

    def __init__(self,provider,leg_encoder_joint_names=None):
        require(provider.order_scope!='unbound','named joint mapping required before learner construction')
        self.provider=provider
        self.leg_encoder_joint_names=tuple(leg_encoder_joint_names or provider.metadata["actuator_leg_joint_names"])
        require(len(self.leg_encoder_joint_names)==4,"four actual leg encoder names required")
        self.leg_ids=[provider.names.index(n) for n in self.leg_encoder_joint_names]
    def to(self,device):return ConvertedMOD4JumpMdp(self.provider.to(device),self.leg_encoder_joint_names)
    def normalize_context(self,x):return normalize_static31(x)
    def add_noise(self,obs):return obs
    def goal(self,frames,num_frames):return self.provider.future_goal(frames,num_frames)
    def relative_features(self,state,frames):
        target,_=self.provider.query(frames)
        shape=frames.shape
        flat=frames.reshape(-1)
        edge=state[0].new_full((flat.numel(),),self.step_edge_x_m)
        top=state[0].new_full((flat.numel(),),self.platform_top_z_m)
        result=task_relative_observation(state[0].reshape(-1,3),target[...,:3].reshape(-1,3),edge,top,flat)
        return result.reshape(*shape,7)
    def obs(self,state,previous_action,context,frames):
        require(len(state)==7,'explicit dynamic state required')
        # Static privileged xi is NOT fed directly to the actor.
        q=state[4].clone()
        q[...,self.leg_ids]+=context[...,27:31]
        observed_state=(*state[:4],q,state[5])
        return torch.cat((invariant_features(observed_state),previous_action,
                          self.relative_features(state,frames)),-1)
    def critic_features(self,state,frames,normalized_context):
        return torch.cat((invariant_features(state[:6]),state[6],normalized_context,
                          self.relative_features(state,frames)),-1)
    def objective_terms(self,state,frames,action,previous_action):
        p,R,v,w,q,dq,aux=state
        target,mask=self.provider.query(frames)
        def masked_mean(error,m):
            return (error*m).sum(-1)/m.sum(-1).clamp_min(1)
        desired_R=quat_to_mat(target[...,3:7])
        pos_error=((p-target[...,:3]).square()*mask[...,:3]).sum(-1)
        ori_error=(R-desired_R).square().mean((-1,-2))
        vel_error=((v-target[...,7:10]).square()*mask[...,7:10]).sum(-1)
        ang_error=masked_mean((w-target[...,10:13]).square(),mask[...,10:13])
        q_error=masked_mean(((q-target[...,13:43])/self.provider.joint_scale).square(),mask[...,13:43])
        dq_error=masked_mean(((dq-target[...,43:73])/(self.provider.joint_scale/.1)).square(),mask[...,43:73])
        terms=dict(position=torch.exp(-pos_error/.3**2),
                   orientation=torch.exp(-ori_error/.4**2),
                   linear_velocity=torch.exp(-vel_error),
                   angular_velocity=torch.exp(-ang_error/3.14**2),
                   joint_position=torch.exp(-q_error),
                   joint_velocity=torch.exp(-dq_error))
        # Additive dense tracking follows the source method's structure, avoiding
        # the previous multiplication of independent near-zero task scores.
        tracking=sum(terms.values())
        action_rate=.1*(action-previous_action).square().sum(-1)
        terms['tracking_sum']=tracking
        terms['action_rate_cost']=action_rate
        terms['total']=(tracking-action_rate)*.01
        return terms
    def reward(self,state,frames,action,previous_action):
        return self.objective_terms(state,frames,action,previous_action)['total']
    def soft_termination(self,state,frames):
        # Original absolute physical low-height/tilt thresholds, using source-style
        # .02/.04 temperatures; actual hard callback truth remains independent.
        height_failure=torch.sigmoid((.12-state[0][...,2])/.02)
        tilt_failure=torch.sigmoid((.3-state[1][...,2,2])/.04)
        return 1-(1-height_failure)*(1-tilt_failure)
    def valid_imagination_start(self,frames,horizon):
        return torch.ones_like(frames,dtype=torch.bool)
    def contract_manifest(self):
        return dict(schema='mod4.native_supermimic.own_converted_jump_mdp.v3',
            physical_joints=30,packed_state=127,dynamic_aux=54,static_xi=31,
            actual_leg_encoder_joint_names=self.leg_encoder_joint_names,world_model_terrain_features=3,
            actor_observation=86,goal_frame=146,future_goal_frames_expected=10,
            reference_order_scope=self.provider.order_scope,
            reference_metadata=self.provider.metadata,
            reward='same additive masked own-reference tracking head on actual and imagined state',
            physical_V8_reward='unchanged original fulltrace, separate from native tracking return',
            position_and_velocity_errors='masked squared coordinate sums',
            soft_survival=dict(height_threshold=.12,tilt_Rzz_threshold=.3,height_temperature=.02,tilt_temperature=.04),
            termination='original hard callbacks unchanged; calibrated analytic native soft approximation',
            successful_skill=False,requires_actual_SDK_source_resource_admission=True)

"""SOURCE CANDIDATE116: direct world command and BOTH wheel-bottom target tracking.

No positive term for force loss, generic flight, body height, tuck or extension.
No action/force/reset controller; these functions return reward scalars only.
Not admitted to any runtime or training. Parameters are engineering proposals.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
import torch

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CommandReward:
    dt:float=.01
    speed:float=.5
    velocity_sigma:float=.35
    step_height:float=.27
    edge_x:float=.35
    far_x:float=1.15
    half_width:float=.6
    goal_root_x:float=.75
    final_brake_distance:float=.2
    near_start:float=.18
    near_full:float=.04
    boundary_ramp:float=.02
    height_coefficient:float=.5
    supported_landing_coefficient:float=2.
    quiet_coefficient:float=1.
    score_scale:float=4.
    quiet_speed_sigma:float=.15

def smooth01(x):
    t=x.clamp(0,1)
    return t*t*(3-2*t)

def modes(low,high,force_z,root_x,p=CommandReward()):
    """Own wheel min/max mesh supports, in meters relative to own world floor.

    Force history is only a declared landing surrogate, never airborne truth.
    Unloaded wheels cannot unlock jump rewards. Both front edges must approach.
    """
    support=force_z.clamp_min(0)/(force_z.clamp_min(0)+1.)
    front_gap=p.edge_x-high[...,0].amin(-1)
    near=smooth01((p.near_start-front_gap)/(p.near_start-p.near_full))
    rear=low[...,0].amin(-1)-p.edge_x
    far=p.far_x-high[...,0].amax(-1)
    yl=low[...,1].amin(-1)+p.half_width
    yr=p.half_width-high[...,1].amax(-1)
    footprint=smooth01(rear/p.boundary_ramp)*smooth01(far/p.boundary_ramp)*smooth01(yl/p.boundary_ramp)*smooth01(yr/p.boundary_ramp)
    top_height=torch.exp(-((low[...,2]-p.step_height)/.02).square().mean(-1))
    P=footprint*top_height*support.prod(-1)
    near=near*smooth01(far/p.boundary_ramp)*smooth01(yl/p.boundary_ramp)*smooth01(yr/p.boundary_ramp)
    base_vx=p.speed*((p.goal_root_x-root_x)/p.final_brake_distance).clamp(-1,1)
    return dict(near=near,top_support=P,footprint=footprint,front_gap_both=front_gap,base_command_vx=base_vx)

def height_score(wheel_bottom2,p=CommandReward()):
    """Target .27m for EACH own wheel bottom, never chassis/COM height.

    Score zero at ground, max ONLY at target, decreases above target.
    The lower of the two target scores prevents rewarding a one-wheel lift.
    No broad high-reward body-height plateau and no positive flight offset.
    """
    u=wheel_bottom2/p.step_height
    each=(2*u-u.square()).clamp_min(0)
    return each.amin(-1)

def evaluate(m,mode,legacy_nonrequest_recovery,effort_quality,p=CommandReward()):
    """Every selected summand is returned verbatim; total is their exact sum.

    effort_quality is a bounded [exp(-.5),1] attenuation derived from the
    retained nonpositive action/torque/limit terms, independent of mode blending.
    A runtime adapter is REQUIRED to supply actual low/high world mesh supports.
    """
    for x in (m['wheel_bottom2'],m['world_velocity3'],m['world_angular_velocity3'],m['request_active'],effort_quality):
        if not bool(torch.isfinite(x).all()):raise ValueError('nonfinite command reward input')
    P=mode['top_support'];near=mode['near'];v=m['world_velocity3']
    desired_x=mode['base_command_vx']*(1-P)
    error=(v[...,0]-desired_x).square()+v[...,1].square()
    velocity=torch.exp(-error/p.velocity_sigma**2)
    heading=(1-m['heading_cos'].clamp(-1,1)).clamp_min(0)
    alignment=torch.exp(-heading/(1-.9800665778412416)-m['root_y'].square()/.3**2)
    # A stationary hop has EXACTLY ZERO height bonus. Slow bouncing has a
    # quadratic small factor; it cannot replace tracking the +.5m/s command.
    forward_fraction=(v[...,0]/p.speed).clamp(0,1).square()
    H=height_score(m['wheel_bottom2'],p)
    quiet=torch.exp(-v[...,:2].square().sum(-1)/p.quiet_speed_sigma**2
        -v[...,2].square()/p.quiet_speed_sigma**2
        -m['world_angular_velocity3'].square().sum(-1)/.3**2
        -(1-m['upright_cos'].clamp(-1,1)).clamp_min(0)/(1-.984807753012208)
        -heading/(1-.9800665778412416))
    active=m['request_active'];k=p.score_scale*p.dt*effort_quality
    selected=dict(
        command_world_velocity=k*velocity*alignment*active,
        both_wheel_height_270_with_forward_command=k*p.height_coefficient*near*(1-P)*H*forward_fraction*alignment*active,
        supported_top_landing=k*p.supported_landing_coefficient*P*active,
        supported_top_quiet=k*p.quiet_coefficient*P*quiet*active,
        original_nonrequest_recovery=(1-active)*legacy_nonrequest_recovery)
    total=sum(selected.values())
    if not bool(torch.isfinite(total).all()):raise ValueError('nonfinite reward')
    return dict(total=total,selected_terms=selected,command_vx=desired_x,command_wheel_bottom_height=p.step_height,
        both_wheel_height_score=H,forward_command_fraction_squared=forward_fraction,
        planning_top_support=P,planning_near=near,quiet=quiet,
        no_airborne_success_inferred=True,not_runtime_admitted=True)

"""Typed flat91 goal encoding, source only until fresh installed admission.

The 1460-vector is ten [value73, known-mask73] frames. Unknown values
must remain absent after normalization; a binary mask is a condition,
not a continuous feature to standardize with an older task's variance.
No module, parameter, buffer, RNG call, action or physical state is added.
"""
import torch

ENCODING = 'mod4.flat91.mask_aware_after_normalization.v1'


def masked_goal_conditioning(normalized, raw_goal):
    if normalized.shape != raw_goal.shape or raw_goal.shape[-1] != 1460:
        raise ValueError('exact ten [value73, binary known-mask73] goals required')
    shape = raw_goal.shape
    raw = raw_goal.reshape(*shape[:-1], 10, 146)
    norm = normalized.reshape(*shape[:-1], 10, 146)
    mask = raw[..., 73:]
    values=norm[..., :73]*mask
    # Goal122 channels43:49 are dimensionless process cues, not physical dq.
    # Fixed .01 scaling avoids arbitrary inherited mean/std in previously
    # unknown slots; all learned norm tensors stay literally unchanged.
    values=torch.cat((values[...,:43],(raw[...,43:49]/.01)*mask[...,43:49],values[...,49:]),-1)
    return torch.cat((values,mask),-1).reshape(shape)


def validate_goal_source(raw_goal):
    """Admission-only check; never installed as a physical-step GPU query."""
    if raw_goal.shape[-1] != 1460 or not torch.isfinite(raw_goal).all():
        raise ValueError('finite typed goal required')
    mask = raw_goal.reshape(*raw_goal.shape[:-1], 10, 146)[..., 73:]
    if not ((mask == 0) | (mask == 1)).all():
        raise ValueError('literal known/unknown mask required')
    return True

"""Bounded mixture of actual replay starts; source candidate, not installed.

Half the requested batch targets the 200ms before takeoff through the requested
flight. The remainder is the original unconditional valid-start distribution.
If the replay lacks enough focused starts, original valid starts fill the batch.
No states, histories, actions, physical resets, rewards or terminals are edited.
Extra original sampler calls consume its RNG; RNG equivalence is not claimed.
"""
import math
import torch
from private_phase_frame_v1 import decode

CONTRACT=dict(schema='mod4.fudan106.actual_replay_prestep_to_recovery20s.v1',numerator=1,denominator=2,maximum_original_sampler_calls=8,actual_age_start=180,actual_age_end_exclusive=2000,policy_hz=100,physical_timeout_ticks=2000,insufficient_focus='fill_with_original_valid_starts',generated_or_modified_transitions=False,original_sampler_RNG_draw_count_unchanged=False)


def focused_mask(frames):
    info=decode(frames);age=info['public_age']
    return info['valid_actual'] & (age>=180) & (age<2000)


def attach_task_focused_starts(learner, *, diagnostic=None):
    assert not hasattr(learner, '_flat95_task_focused_starts')
    original = learner._sample_valid_starts
    totals = dict(calls=0, original_sampler_calls=0, requested_focus=0,
                  selected_focus=0, fallback_uniform=0, returned_starts=0)

    def sample(batch_size):
        assert type(batch_size) is int and batch_size > 0
        uniform = original(batch_size)
        requested = batch_size // 2
        starts, histories, count, draws = [], [], 0, 1
        candidate = uniform
        while True:
            start, history = candidate
            indices = focused_mask(start['f']).nonzero(as_tuple=False).squeeze(-1)
            indices = indices[:requested-count]
            if len(indices):
                starts.append({k: v[indices] for k, v in start.items()})
                histories.append({k: v[indices] for k, v in history.items()})
                count += len(indices)
            if count == requested or draws == 8:
                break
            candidate = original(batch_size)
            draws += 1
        # Whole rows and their matching histories stay paired. The original
        # uniform sample also supplies a truthful fallback when focus is absent.
        remaining = batch_size-count
        def combine(parts, base):
            return {k: torch.cat([*(p[k] for p in parts), v[:remaining]], dim=0)
                    for k, v in base.items()}
        result = (combine(starts, uniform[0]), combine(histories, uniform[1]))
        totals['calls'] += 1
        totals['original_sampler_calls'] += draws
        totals['requested_focus'] += requested
        totals['selected_focus'] += count
        totals['fallback_uniform'] += requested-count
        totals['returned_starts'] += batch_size
        if diagnostic is not None:
            diagnostic(dict(totals, last=dict(batch_size=batch_size,
                       requested_focus=requested, selected_focus=count,
                       fallback_uniform=requested-count, original_calls=draws)))
        return result

    learner._sample_valid_starts = sample
    learner._flat95_task_focused_starts = totals
    return totals

"""Own low-jump curriculum math only; not installed or a physical success gate.

The real video informs extension/tuck/landing phases, not measured MOD4 joints.
Inputs must come from the actual own wheel FK/forces. A future imagined adapter
must advance its phase and update dynamic contact context on every rollout step.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
import math
import torch

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FlatJumpPhase:
    height_m: float = .05
    control_dt_s: float = .01
    initial_standing_s: float = 2.
    repeat_s: float = 2.5
    stable_recovery_s: float = .5
    gravity_m_s2: float = 9.81

    def __post_init__(self):
        assert self.height_m in (.05, .10, .20, .27)
        assert self.control_dt_s in (.01, .02)
        assert self.initial_standing_s >= self.stable_recovery_s

    def targets(self, ages, env_ids):
        """Stagger first requests, never start an environment in a fake air pose.

        Uses deterministic IDs, no RNG draws or physical reset modifications.
        Actual integration must bind IDs/age after each genuine reset.
        """
        age = ages.to(dtype=torch.float32) * self.control_dt_s
        period_ticks = round(self.repeat_s / self.control_dt_s)
        delay = torch.remainder(env_ids, period_ticks).to(age.dtype) * self.control_dt_s
        elapsed = age - self.initial_standing_s - delay
        active = elapsed >= 0
        phase = torch.remainder(elapsed.clamp_min(0), self.repeat_s)
        v0 = math.sqrt(2 * self.gravity_m_s2 * self.height_m)
        flight_s = 2 * v0 / self.gravity_m_s2
        flight = active & (phase <= flight_s)
        height = torch.where(flight, (v0*phase - .5*self.gravity_m_s2*phase.square()).clamp_min(0), torch.zeros_like(phase))
        vertical_v = torch.where(flight, v0-self.gravity_m_s2*phase, torch.zeros_like(phase))
        return dict(height=height, vertical_v=vertical_v, flight=flight,
                    ground=~flight, phase_s=phase, active=active,
                    nominal_flight_s=flight_s)


def huber(x):
    a=x.abs()
    return torch.where(a <= 1, .5*x.square(), a-.5)


def flat_jump_terms(wheel_bottom_m, wheel_vertical_v, Rzz, root_vxy,
                    ground_support_probability, target, action, previous_action):
    """Task reward on real or predicted OWN measurements, not a forced path.

    ground_support_probability must be a live current-step wheel support signal
    from the admitted real/imagined adapter. No constant contact assumption.
    Reward positivity avoids rewarding early termination through negative tails.
    """
    assert wheel_bottom_m.shape[-1] == wheel_vertical_v.shape[-1] == 2
    assert ground_support_probability.shape == wheel_bottom_m.shape
    assert torch.isfinite(ground_support_probability).all()
    assert ((ground_support_probability >= 0) & (ground_support_probability <= 1)).all()
    h=target['height'][...,None];vz=target['vertical_v'][...,None]
    height_cost=huber((wheel_bottom_m-h)/.02).mean(-1)
    velocity_cost=huber((wheel_vertical_v-vz)/.5).mean(-1)
    # The original actual hard orientation limit is.75rad, not acos(.3).
    tilt_cost=huber((1-Rzz)/(1-math.cos(.75)))
    ground=target['ground'].to(wheel_bottom_m.dtype)
    support_target=ground[...,None].expand_as(ground_support_probability)
    support_cost=(ground_support_probability-support_target).square().mean(-1)
    ground_drift_cost=huber(root_vxy/.15).mean(-1)*ground
    action_rate=(action-previous_action).square().mean(-1)
    cost=4*height_cost+velocity_cost+4*tilt_cost+2*support_cost+ground_drift_cost+action_rate/6
    return dict(height_cost=height_cost,tilt_cost=tilt_cost,support_cost=support_cost,
                total=.06/(1+cost),nonnegative_cost=cost,
                tilt_failure_probability=torch.sigmoid((math.cos(.75)-Rzz)/.04))


def physical_skill_scope():
    return dict(first_training_target_wheel_bottom_m=.05,
        later_height_targets_m=[.10,.20,.27],
        flat_acceptance='actual both-wheel clearance, actual landing support and >=200 stable2.5ms callbacks',
        step_final_acceptance='unchanged270mm cross/fullfootprint/topcontact12+stable200callbacks',
        not_COM_height_or_imagined_reward_success=True,
        source_math_only_not_installed=True,
        actual_FK_force_basis_phase_advance_context_refresh_SDK_and_runtime_pending=True,
        no_new_Fudan_joint_mocap_or_dynamic_feasibility_claim=True)

"""Optional sampled-start accounting per Pi call, not per physical control.

This records attempted imagination starts, never successful optimizer updates or
physical task events. It preserves original sampling/actions/reward and RNG.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import json
import math


class NativePolicyPhaseCoverage:
    def __init__(self, path, *, window_start_iteration):
        self.path = Path(path)
        self.window_start_iteration = int(window_start_iteration)
        self.calls = 0
        self.samples = 0
        self.bands = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0]
        self.reaches_request = 0
        self.errors = []
        self.valid = True
        self.last = None
        self.io_errors = []

    @staticmethod
    def _counts(frames, horizon):
        if type(horizon) is not int or not 1<=horizon<=32:
            raise ValueError('invalid flat imagination horizon')
        bands=[0,0,0,0,0];reaches=0
        flight_ticks=2*math.sqrt(2*9.81*.05)/9.81/.01
        for frame in frames:
            if type(frame) is not int or frame<0:
                raise ValueError('invalid private sampled frame')
            delay,age=divmod(frame,1024)
            if not 0<=delay<250 or not 0<=age<=500:
                raise ValueError('private sampled delay/actual age invalid')
            relative=age-200-delay
            phase=relative%250
            index=0 if age<200 else 1 if relative<0 else 2 if phase<=flight_ticks else 3 if phase<=flight_ticks+50 else 4
            bands[index]+=1
            for onset in (200+delay,450+delay):
                if max(age+1,onset)<=min(age+horizon,onset+flight_ticks):
                    reaches+=1;break
        return bands,reaches

    def snapshot(self):
        return dict(schema='mod4.native.flat_private_phase_sampling.v1',
                    diagnostic_valid=self.valid, window_start_iteration=self.window_start_iteration,
                    policy_sampling_calls=self.calls, sampled_starts=self.samples,
                    bands=dict(zip(('initial_standing', 'waiting_staggered_request', 'requested_flight', 'landing_stabilize', 'inter_attempt_idle'), self.bands)),
                    imagined_horizon_intersects_request=self.reaches_request, private_frame_stride=1024, requested_height_m=.05, actual_100Hz_episode_bound=500,
                    last=self.last, errors=list(self.errors), io_errors=list(self.io_errors),
                    counts_attempted_starts_not_completed_optimizer_updates=True,
                    not_a_training_or_model_or_skill_gate=True,
                    extra_GPU_transfer_only_once_per_policy_sampling_call_not_per_control=True,
                    no_sampling_distribution_action_reward_or_RNG_changes=True)

    def _persist(self):
        try:
            self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            temporary = self.path.with_suffix(self.path.suffix + '.partial')
            temporary.write_text(json.dumps(self.snapshot(), indent=2), encoding='utf8')
            temporary.replace(self.path)
        except Exception as error:
            # Optional I/O is never allowed to hide a physical/optimizer failure.
            self.io_errors.append(type(error).__name__ + ': ' + str(error))
            self.io_errors[:] = self.io_errors[-8:]

    def note_error(self, error):
        self.valid = False
        self.errors.append(type(error).__name__ + ': ' + str(error))
        self.errors[:] = self.errors[-8:]
        self._persist()

    def record(self, frames, *, iteration, horizon):
        try:
            bands, reaches = self._counts(frames, horizon)
            if iteration < self.window_start_iteration:
                raise ValueError('iteration regressed before window start')
            self.calls += 1
            self.samples += len(frames)
            self.bands = [a + b for a, b in zip(self.bands, bands)]
            self.reaches_request += reaches
            self.last = dict(iteration=int(iteration), count=len(frames), min=min(frames) if frames else None,
                             max=max(frames) if frames else None, bands=bands, horizon=int(horizon))
            self._persist()
        except Exception as error:
            self.note_error(error)


def attach_optional_policy_phase_coverage(learner, path):
    """Wrap only the existing sample result, with identical object forwarding.

    Original exceptions escape unchanged. The additional frame transfer occurs
    only when Pi requests its existing start sample, outside physical controls.
    It is optional instrumentation with its own truthful invalid/error state.
    """
    if hasattr(learner, '_mod4_native_phase_coverage'):
        raise ValueError('phase coverage already attached')
    original = learner._sample_valid_starts
    diagnostic = NativePolicyPhaseCoverage(path, window_start_iteration=learner.iteration)

    def sample(batch_size):
        result = original(batch_size)
        try:
            frames = result[0]['f'].detach().cpu().tolist()
            diagnostic.record(frames, iteration=int(learner.iteration), horizon=int(learner.cfg.horizon))
        except Exception as error:
            diagnostic.note_error(error)
        return result

    learner._sample_valid_starts = sample
    learner._mod4_native_phase_coverage = diagnostic
    return diagnostic

"""Independent flat-jump diagnostics from existing real physics callback tensors.

No actor, force, query, RNG, reward, reset, geometry or old270 goal modification.
Support truth is supplied by an admitted floor/contact provider, never an AABB.
"""
import math
import torch

class FlatRealPhysicsEvents:
 def __init__(self,n,device='cpu',height_m=.05):
  assert n>0 and height_m in (.05,.10,.20,.27)
  self.n=n;self.height_m=height_m;self.physics_dt_s=.0025;self.last_tick=None;self.callback_rows=0;self.tick_errors=0
  self.diagnostic_errors={}
  for k in ('attempt_generation','reset_generation','stable_ticks','air_ticks','landing_ticks'):
   setattr(self,k,torch.zeros(n,dtype=torch.long,device=device))
  for k in ('active','seen_ground','positive_vz','had_takeoff','height_met','landed','credited','previous_request','attempt_invalid','previous_ground'):
   setattr(self,k,torch.zeros(n,dtype=torch.bool,device=device))
  self.yaw_reference=torch.zeros(n,device=device)
  self.lifetime={k:torch.zeros(n,dtype=torch.long,device=device) for k in ('attempts','takeoffs','height_met','landings','recoveries','selected_resets','reset_censored_attempts','invalid_env_callbacks')}
 def _clear(self,mask):
  for k in ('active','seen_ground','positive_vz','had_takeoff','height_met','landed','credited','attempt_invalid'):
   getattr(self,k)[mask]=False
  for k in ('stable_ticks','air_ticks','landing_ticks'):getattr(self,k)[mask]=0
  self.yaw_reference[mask]=0
 def reset(self,ids):
  # Lifetime events are incremented at physics time and survive selected reset.
  self.lifetime['reset_censored_attempts'][ids]+=(self.active[ids]&~self.credited[ids]).long()
  self.lifetime['selected_resets'][ids]+=1;self.reset_generation[ids]+=1
  self._clear(ids);self.previous_request[ids]=False;self.previous_ground[ids]=False
 def note_error(self,error):
  key=type(error).__name__+': '+str(error);self.diagnostic_errors[key]=self.diagnostic_errors.get(key,0)+1
  self.attempt_invalid|=self.active;self.stable_ticks.zero_()
 def physical(self,*,physics_tick,request,wheel_bottom_m,wheel_N,wheel_floor_support,
              floor_basis_valid,all_min_clear_m,nonwheel_max_N,root_vz,
              root_planar_speed,roll,pitch,yaw,attempt_start=None):
  assert type(physics_tick)is int and physics_tick>=1
  vectors=(request,floor_basis_valid,all_min_clear_m,nonwheel_max_N,root_vz,root_planar_speed,roll,pitch,yaw)
  assert all(x.shape==(self.n,) for x in vectors)
  assert wheel_bottom_m.shape==wheel_N.shape==wheel_floor_support.shape==(self.n,2)
  assert request.dtype==floor_basis_valid.dtype==wheel_floor_support.dtype==torch.bool
  if self.last_tick is not None and physics_tick!=self.last_tick+1:
   self.tick_errors+=1;self.attempt_invalid|=self.active;self.stable_ticks.zero_()
  self.last_tick=physics_tick;self.callback_rows+=1
  fresh=(request&~self.previous_request) if attempt_start is None else attempt_start
  assert fresh.shape==(self.n,) and fresh.dtype==torch.bool
  ground_before=self.previous_ground&fresh
  self._clear(fresh);self.active|=fresh;self.attempt_generation+=fresh.long();self.lifetime['attempts']+=fresh.long()
  self.yaw_reference=torch.where(fresh,yaw,self.yaw_reference);self.previous_request.copy_(request)
  finite=torch.isfinite(wheel_bottom_m).all(-1)&torch.isfinite(wheel_N).all(-1)
  for x in vectors[2:]:finite&=torch.isfinite(x)
  valid=finite&floor_basis_valid
  self.lifetime['invalid_env_callbacks']+=(~valid).long();self.attempt_invalid|=self.active&~valid
  eligible=self.active&~self.attempt_invalid&valid
  clean=nonwheel_max_N<=1.
  floor=wheel_floor_support.all(-1)&clean&(all_min_clear_m>=-.001)
  self.seen_ground|=eligible&(floor|ground_before)&~self.had_takeoff
  self.previous_ground.copy_(valid&floor)
  self.positive_vz|=eligible&(root_vz>.05)
  airborne=(wheel_N<=1.).all(-1)&(wheel_bottom_m>.005).all(-1)&(all_min_clear_m>0)&clean
  air=eligible&self.seen_ground&self.positive_vz&airborne
  self.air_ticks=torch.where(air,self.air_ticks+1,0)
  takeoff=air&(self.air_ticks>=16)&~self.had_takeoff;self.had_takeoff|=takeoff
  height=eligible&self.had_takeoff&airborne&(wheel_bottom_m>=self.height_m).all(-1)&~self.height_met
  self.height_met|=height
  self.landing_ticks=torch.where(eligible&self.had_takeoff&self.height_met&floor,self.landing_ticks+1,0)
  landing=(self.landing_ticks>=12)&~self.landed;self.landed|=landing
  dyaw=torch.atan2(torch.sin(yaw-self.yaw_reference),torch.cos(yaw-self.yaw_reference))
  stable=eligible&self.landed&floor&(root_vz.abs()<=.03)&(root_planar_speed<=.03)&(roll.abs()<math.radians(10))&(pitch.abs()<math.radians(10))&(dyaw.abs()<.2)
  self.stable_ticks=torch.where(stable,self.stable_ticks+1,0)
  recovery=(self.stable_ticks>=200)&~self.credited;self.credited|=recovery
  for k,event in (('takeoffs',takeoff),('height_met',height),('landings',landing),('recoveries',recovery)):self.lifetime[k]+=event.long()
  return dict(takeoff=takeoff,height_met=height,landing=landing,recovery=recovery)
 def snapshot(self):
  # Invoke only at normal report/save boundaries, not at each GPU callback.
  return dict(schema='mod4.flat_jump.real_physics_event_diagnostic.v1',height_m=self.height_m,physics_dt_s=self.physics_dt_s,
   callbacks=self.callback_rows,last_physics_tick=self.last_tick,tick_errors=self.tick_errors,
   lifetime={k:int(v.sum().detach().cpu()) for k,v in self.lifetime.items()},
   per_env_lifetime={k:v.detach().cpu().tolist() for k,v in self.lifetime.items()},
   diagnostic_errors=dict(self.diagnostic_errors),stable_required_callbacks=200,stable_required_s=.5,
   takeoff_callbacks=16,landing_callbacks=12,attempt_credit_cleared_on_new_attempt=True,
   lifetime_events_survive_selected_reset=True,not_270_goal_or_reward_or_skill_promotion=True)

def install_readonly_phase_hook(phase,cache_provider,*,height_m=.05):
 """Call old physical/reset once; provider reads only same-tick existing caches.

 cache_provider(phase, kw) -> physical() kwargs excluding request by default.
 It must provide qualified floor_basis_valid and true wheel_floor_support.
 It may not cast/query, fetch forces, run actor/FK, sample RNG or step physics.
 """
 assert not hasattr(phase,'_flat_real_physics_counter')
 counter=FlatRealPhysicsEvents(phase.n,phase.device,height_m);old_physical=phase.physical;old_reset=phase.reset
 def physical(**kw):
  result=old_physical(**kw)  # Original errors and return object are untouched.
  try:
   sample=cache_provider(phase,kw)
   sample.setdefault('request',phase.request_for_action)
   counter.physical(**sample)
  except Exception as error:counter.note_error(error)  # Diagnostic cannot replace old execution.
  return result
 def reset(ids):
  result=old_reset(ids)  # Preserve original selected-reset ordering and error.
  try:counter.reset(ids)
  except Exception as error:counter.note_error(error)
  return result
 phase.physical=physical;phase.reset=reset;phase._flat_real_physics_counter=counter
 return counter

def admitted_plane_cached_provider(phase,kw,*,admission):
 """Optional zero-query provider for an actually admitted floor-only z=0 scene.

 A cached net force or wheel AABB alone cannot certify its contact surface.
 Root must supply the real one-time scene/collision-domain admission receipt.
 No contact-pair/face-manifold identity claim is made by this provider.
 """
 assert admission['actual_admitted_flat_floor_only_collision_domain'] is True
 assert admission['floor_z_m']==0. and len(admission['actual_scene_admission_receipt_SHA'])==64
 env=phase.env;bottom=kw['wheel_clear_m']
 # This is the existing 5ms contact cache already updated before the callback.
 wheel_up=env._integrated_contact.history[:,0,:,2]
 support=(wheel_up>1.)&(bottom>=-.001)
 return dict(physics_tick=env.h1_callback_count+1,wheel_bottom_m=bottom,wheel_N=kw['wheel_N'],
  wheel_floor_support=support,floor_basis_valid=torch.ones_like(phase.request_for_action),
  **{k:kw[k] for k in ('all_min_clear_m','nonwheel_max_N','root_vz','root_planar_speed','roll','pitch','yaw')})

def make_authored_preedge_cached_provider(phase):
 """Existing step scene: real floor-face ray + complete preedge footprint + Fz.

 The existing scene initializer already compares actual stage ordered triangles
 to the authored generator. That generator's first faces0,1 are floor z=0.
 AABB is used only for footprint/boundary position, not to invent face identity.
 SignedFz is the existing5ms contact mean, not an instantaneous manifold.
 """
 env=phase.env;receipt=env.step270_geometry_receipt;geometry=receipt['geometry']
 assert geometry==dict(height=.270,edge_x=.35,depth=.8,width=1.2)
 assert len(receipt['actual_stage_ordered_triangle_SHA'])==64
 origins=env.scene.env_origins
 floor_lower=origins[:,:2].amin(0)-2.5;floor_upper=origins[:,:2].amax(0)+2.5
 edge=origins[:,0,None]+geometry['edge_x']
 def provider(current,kw):
  assert current is phase
  cache=phase.last_measured;lo=cache['step_wheel_min'];hi=cache['step_wheel_max']
  face=cache['step_query_face'];height=cache['step_query_height'];bottom=kw['wheel_clear_m']
  # All these values were computed by the same original physical callback.
  floor_face=(face==0)|(face==1)
  on_floor_ray=floor_face&(height.abs()<=.001)
  complete_inside=((lo[:,:,:2]>=floor_lower)&(hi[:,:,:2]<=floor_upper)).all(-1)
  preedge=hi[:,:,0]<edge
  location=complete_inside&preedge&on_floor_ray
  signed_Fz_5ms=env._integrated_contact.history[:,0,:,2]
  support=location&(signed_Fz_5ms>1.)&(bottom>=-.001)
  return dict(physics_tick=env.h1_callback_count+1,wheel_bottom_m=bottom,wheel_N=kw['wheel_N'],
   wheel_floor_support=support,floor_basis_valid=location.all(-1),
   **{k:kw[k] for k in ('all_min_clear_m','nonwheel_max_N','root_vz','root_planar_speed','roll','pitch','yaw')})
 provider.floor_support_basis=dict(actual_scene_ordered_triangle_SHA=receipt['actual_stage_ordered_triangle_SHA'],
  authored_floor_face_ids=[0,1],authored_floor_z_m=0.,complete_wheel_footprint_before_edge=True,
  force_basis='existing integrated_contact.history[:,0,:,2] signedFz5ms mean >1N',
  instantaneous_contact_manifold_or_pair_ID_claimed=False,new_query_or_force_fetch=False)
 return provider

"""Explicit closed-PPO-policy bootstrap with a NEW4096 physical episode.

No old20k environmental state is reshaped, restored or labeled exact continuation.
The original fullsave chain is installed separately on the actual new environment.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,json,time
import torch

BOOTSTRAP_SHA='0c1edb9603c1cb84fbb894d242dae1b6a7c87fe3237d658dbc486ccbbad3d394'
BOOTSTRAP_ADAM=832460

def exact(a,b):
    if torch.is_tensor(a):return torch.is_tensor(b) and a.dtype==b.dtype and a.shape==b.shape and torch.equal(a.cpu(),b.cpu())
    if isinstance(a,dict):return isinstance(b,dict) and set(a)==set(b) and all(exact(a[k],b[k]) for k in a)
    if isinstance(a,(list,tuple)):return type(a)==type(b) and len(a)==len(b) and all(exact(x,y) for x,y in zip(a,b))
    return type(a)==type(b) and a==b

def verify_loaded_policy_only_fresh_environment(runner,env,checkpoint):
    if env.num_envs!=4096 or env.physics_dt!=.0025 or env.step_dt!=.01 or env.cfg.decimation!=4:
        raise ValueError('explicit4096/original2.5ms100Hz required')
    if float(env.cfg.episode_length_s)!=20.:
        raise ValueError('explicit user20second practice episodes required')
    if int(env._sim_step_counter)!=0 or bool(env.h1_phase.age_control.any()):
        raise ValueError('new physical episode must start at zero control clock')
    if bool(env.h1_phase.goal.success.any()) or bool(env.h1_phase.crossed.any()):
        raise ValueError('new episode cannot inherit success credits')
    pairs=[('actor_state_dict',runner.alg.actor.state_dict()),
           ('critic_state_dict',runner.alg.critic.state_dict()),
           ('optimizer_state_dict',runner.alg.optimizer.state_dict())]
    for name,current in pairs:
        if not exact(current,checkpoint[name]):raise ValueError('policy bootstrap differs: '+name)
    if len(checkpoint['actor_state_dict'])!=9 or len(checkpoint['critic_state_dict'])!=8:
        raise ValueError('whole qualified actor/critic groups required')
    if checkpoint['actor_state_dict']['distribution.std_param'].shape!=(6,):
        raise ValueError('all six STD required')
    if len(checkpoint['optimizer_state_dict']['state'])!=17 or {int(x['step']) for x in checkpoint['optimizer_state_dict']['state'].values()}!={BOOTSTRAP_ADAM}:
        raise ValueError('exact qualified17Adam832460 required')
    # Validate each new component's own complete state and target population;
    # do not compare it to saved20k environmental tensors.
    states={
        'sensor':env._sensor_mild.state_dict(),
        'physical_queue':env._physics_action_delay.state_dict(),
        'spring':env._spring_pressure.state_dict(),
        'gas':env._gas_damping.state_dict(),
        'COM':env._chassis_com.state_dict(),
        'PD':env._explicit_pd.state_dict(),
    }
    for name,value in states.items():
        count=value.get('n',value.get('num_envs'))
        if count!=4096:raise ValueError('new physical component population differs: '+name)
    return dict(policy_actor9_critic8_STD6_17Adam832460_exact=True,
        native_architecture_and_optimizers_fresh=True,physical_episode_fresh=True,
        old_environment_population=20000,new_environment_population=4096,
        old20k_environmental_state_restored=False,old_native86_optimizer_restored=False,
        PhysX_exact_resume=False,actual_clock_zero=True,actual_component_populations4096=True,
        source_role='closed_production_policy_only_bootstrap_for_new_native_method')

def resume_policy_only_fresh4096(runner,env,path,log_dir):
    path=Path(path)
    digest=hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    if digest!=BOOTSTRAP_SHA:raise ValueError('latest closedH1 policy sourceSHA differs')
    checkpoint=torch.load(path,map_location='cpu',weights_only=False)
    row=verify_loaded_policy_only_fresh_environment(runner,env,checkpoint)
    row.update(epoch=time.time(),source_SHA=digest,source_Adam=BOOTSTRAP_ADAM)
    out=Path(log_dir)/'NATIVE87_POLICY_ONLY_FRESH4096_BOOTSTRAP_ACTUAL.json'
    if out.exists():raise FileExistsError(out)
    out.write_text(json.dumps(row,indent=2),'utf8')
    return row

def scalar_spans(text):
 """JSON scalar offsets with named paths, rejecting duplicate object keys."""
 dec=json.JSONDecoder();spans={}
 def space(i):
  while i<len(text) and text[i].isspace():i+=1
  return i
 def walk(i,path):
  i=space(i)
  if text[i]=='{':
   i=space(i+1);seen=set()
   if text[i]=='}':return i+1
   while True:
    key,j=dec.raw_decode(text,i);require(type(key) is str and key not in seen,'duplicate/invalid JSON key');seen.add(key);i=space(j);require(text[i]==':','JSON colon');i=walk(i+1,path+(key,));i=space(i)
    if text[i]=='}':return i+1
    require(text[i]==',','JSON comma');i=space(i+1)
  if text[i]=='[':
   i=space(i+1);index=0
   if text[i]==']':return i+1
   while True:
    i=walk(i,path+(index,));index+=1;i=space(i)
    if text[i]==']':return i+1
    require(text[i]==',','JSON array comma');i=space(i+1)
  value,end=dec.raw_decode(text,i);spans[path]=(i,end,value);return end
 end=walk(0,());require(space(end)==len(text),'JSON trailing data');return spans

"""Standalone H1 target-conditioned SuperMimic experiment on the original Isaac plant.



Actual physical success remains the existing callback state machine. The analytic

root reference is an experimental objective, not a successful motion demonstration.

"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
