class OwnCompleteSavedStop(Exception):
    pass

def put(path, value):
    path = Path(path)
    tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + '.partial')
    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2), 'utf8')
    tmp.replace(path)

class H1GoalMdp:
    num_joints = 30
    action_dim = 6
    context_dim = 49
    obs_dim = 128
    goal_frame_dim = 73
    max_step_reward = 1.0

    def __init__(self, nominal_joints, nominal_height):
        self.nominal_joints = nominal_joints.detach().clone()
        self.nominal_height = float(nominal_height)

    def to(self, device):
        return H1GoalMdp(self.nominal_joints.to(device), self.nominal_height)

    def normalize_context(self, context):
        return torch.tanh(context)

    def add_noise(self, obs):
        return obs

    def obs(self, state, previous_action, context, frames):
        return torch.cat((invariant_features(state), previous_action, self.normalize_context(context)), -1)

    def goal(self, frames, num_frames):
        goals = []
        for offset in range(num_frames):
            f = frames.float() + offset
            t = ((f - 100) / 45).clamp(0, 1)
            smooth = t * t * (3 - 2 * t)
            x = 0.65 * smooth
            z = self.nominal_height + 0.27 * smooth + 0.3 * torch.sin(torch.pi * t)
            result = torch.zeros((*frames.shape, 73), device=frames.device, dtype=torch.float32)
            result[..., 0] = x
            result[..., 2] = z
            result[..., 3] = 1.0
            result[..., 13:43] = self.nominal_joints
            goals.append(result)
        return torch.cat(goals, -1)

    def reward(self, state, frames, action, previous_action):
        position, rotation, vel, ang, joint, jointvel = state
        target = self.goal(frames, 1)
        tracking = torch.exp(-5 * (position[..., 0] - target[..., 0]).square() - 5 * position[..., 1].square() - 15 * (position[..., 2] - target[..., 2]).square())
        upright = ((rotation[..., 2, 2] + 1) * 0.5).clamp(0, 1)
        return tracking * upright - 0.001 * (action - previous_action).square().mean(-1) - 0.001 * vel.square().mean(-1) - 0.0001 * jointvel.square().mean(-1)

    def soft_termination(self, state, frames):
        return (torch.sigmoid((0.12 - state[0][..., 2]) * 20) + torch.sigmoid((0.3 - state[1][..., 2, 2]) * 10)).clamp(0, 1)

    def critic_features(self, state, frames, normalized_context):
        return torch.cat((invariant_features(state), normalized_context, (frames.float() / 200)[..., None]), -1)

    def valid_imagination_start(self, frames, horizon):
        return torch.ones_like(frames, dtype=torch.bool)

@dataclass
class BridgeState:
    info: dict
    reward: torch.Tensor
    terminated: torch.Tensor
    truncated: torch.Tensor
    obs: dict

class H1Bridge:

    def __init__(self, runner, out, go):
        self.runner = runner
        self.vec = runner.env
        self.real = self.vec.unwrapped
        self.num_envs = self.real.num_envs
        self.device = torch.device(self.real.device)
        self.out = Path(out)
        self.go = go
        self.state = None
        self.trace = None
        self.learner = None
        self.ep_step = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device, dtype=torch.int32)
        self.pi_updates = 0
        self.wm_updates = 0
        self.last_wm = None
        self.last_pi = None
        self.last_curve = -1
        robot = self.real.scene['robot']
        height = float((robot.data.root_pos_w[:, 2] - self.real.scene.env_origins[:, 2]).median())
        self.mdp = H1GoalMdp(robot.data.default_joint_pos[0], height)

    @property
    def supermimic_mdp(self):
        return self.mdp

    @torch.inference_mode()
    def pack(self, root, joint, velocity):
        root = root.detach().clone()
        root[:, :3] -= self.real.scene.env_origins
        root[:, 3:7] = root[:, [6, 3, 4, 5]]
        return torch.cat((root, joint.detach(), velocity.detach()), -1)

    @torch.inference_mode()
    def current(self):
        r = self.real.scene['robot']
        s = self.pack(r.data.root_state_w, r.data.joint_pos, r.data.joint_vel)
        ctx = torch.cat((self.real._integrated_contact.history.flatten(1), r.data.applied_torque, self.real.h1_phase.public_request().float()[:, None]), -1).detach().clone()
        return (s, ctx)

    def init_state(self):
        s, ctx = self.current()
        zero = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
        self.state = BridgeState(info={SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY: dict(s=s, xi=ctx, f=self.real.h1_phase.age_control.clone(), a_prev=torch.zeros(self.num_envs, 6, device=self.device), ep_step=self.ep_step.clone())}, reward=zero, terminated=zero.bool(), truncated=zero.bool(), obs={})
        return self.state

    @torch.inference_mode()
    def before_action(self):
        if time.time() >= self.go['save_deadline'] or (self.out / 'STOP_AFTER_SAVE.json').exists() or shutil.disk_usage(self.out).free < 8 * 1024 ** 3:
            raise OwnCompleteSavedStop()
        assert self.trace is not None
        self.runner.alg.act(self.vec.get_observations())
        if self.learner.iteration != self.last_curve:
            events = self.trace.lifetime_goal_counter.snapshot()
            row = dict(epoch=time.time(), GO_elapsed_s=time.time() - self.go['learning_started_epoch'], native_iteration=self.learner.iteration, world_updates=self.wm_updates, policy_updates=self.pi_updates, environment_transitions=self.trace.total * self.num_envs, completed_control_rows=self.trace.total, events=events, wm_last=self.last_wm, policy_last=self.last_pi, not_frozen_evaluation_or_causal_PPO_comparison=True)
            with (self.out / 'ACTUAL_H1_CONVERGENCE_CURVE.jsonl').open('a', encoding='utf8') as f:
                f.write(json.dumps(row) + '\n')
            self.last_curve = self.learner.iteration
            if self.pi_updates > self.window_start_pi_updates:
                checkpoint = self.trace.source_models[-1]
                positive = dict(checkpoint, updates=self.learner.iteration, world_updates=self.wm_updates, policy_updates=self.pi_updates, original_task_events=events, not_PPO_or_production_source=True)
                put(self.out / 'latest_complete.json', positive)
                if not (self.out / 'FIRST_NATIVE_SUPERMIMIC_POLICY_UPDATE_POSITIVE_ACTUAL.json').exists():
                    put(self.out / 'FIRST_NATIVE_SUPERMIMIC_POLICY_UPDATE_POSITIVE_ACTUAL.json', positive)

    @torch.inference_mode()
    def step(self, actions):
        info = self.state.info[SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY]
        obs, reward, done, extras = self.vec.step(actions)
        frame = self.trace.frame
        next_before_reset = self.pack(frame['root13'], frame['q30'], frame['joint_velocity'])
        executed = frame['executed6'].detach().clone()
        transition = dict(s=info['s'], s1=next_before_reset, a=executed, a_prev=info['a_prev'], xi=info['xi'], r=reward.detach(), f=info['f'], ep_step=info['ep_step'], done=done.bool())
        s, ctx = self.current()
        self.ep_step = torch.where(done.bool(), 0, self.ep_step + 1).to(torch.int32)
        previous = executed.clone()
        previous[done.bool()] = 0
        stateinfo = dict(s=s, xi=ctx, f=self.real.h1_phase.age_control.clone(), a_prev=previous, ep_step=self.ep_step.clone())
        self.state = BridgeState(info={SUPERMIMIC_STATE_KEY: stateinfo, SUPERMIMIC_TRANSITION_KEY: transition}, reward=reward, terminated=done.bool(), truncated=torch.zeros_like(done, dtype=torch.bool), obs={})
        return self.state

def run(runner, n, log_dir, B, J, out, task, go, src, phase):
    B, J, out = (Path(B), Path(J), Path(out))
    runner.logger.writer = None
    env = runner.env.unwrapped
    assert env.num_envs == 20000 and env.physics_dt == 0.0025 and (env.step_dt == 0.01) and (env.cfg.decimation == 4)
    from spring_pressure_checkpoint import exact
    source = torch.load(src, map_location='cpu', weights_only=False)
    for key, value in [('actor_state_dict', runner.alg.actor.state_dict()), ('critic_state_dict', runner.alg.critic.state_dict()), ('optimizer_state_dict', runner.alg.optimizer.state_dict())]:
        assert exact(value, source[key]), key
    assert {int(v['step']) for v in source['optimizer_state_dict']['state'].values()} == {task['source_Adamstep']}
    assert not env.h1_phase.age_control.any()
    from h1_reward_scope_v1 import isolated_actual_SDK_dt_probe
    sdk = isolated_actual_SDK_dt_probe(env)
    bridge = H1Bridge(runner, out, go)
    # New reference is a measured foreign jump retargeted to our static geometry;
    # its phase masks/task support objective are distinct from physical rewards.
    from supermimic_reference_adapter_v1 import read_json_reference, PartialReferenceImitationMdp
    reference_bundle = json.loads((B / 'MIXED84_REFERENCE_BINDING_ACTUAL.json').read_bytes())
    reference_path = B / reference_bundle['reference']['artifact_relative']
    assert hashlib.sha256(reference_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == reference_bundle['reference']['reference_artifact_SHA']
    reference = read_json_reference(reference_path)
    actual_joint_names = list(env.scene['robot'].joint_names)
    reference = reference.for_sdk_joint_order(actual_joint_names)
    assert reference.metadata['sdk_joint_order_verified'] is True
    bridge.mdp = PartialReferenceImitationMdp(bridge.mdp, reference.torch_provider(env.device))
    runtime_objective = bridge.mdp.contract_manifest()
    assert runtime_objective['provider_execution_scope'] == 'runtime_actual_SDK_named_mapping_required'
    assert runtime_objective['reference_metadata']['sdk_joint_order_verified'] is True
    assert runtime_objective['config'] == reference_bundle['objective_contract']['config']
    runtime_bytes = (json.dumps(runtime_objective, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')) + '\n').encode('utf8')
    runtime_contract_SHA = hashlib.sha256(runtime_bytes).hexdigest()
    runtime_path = out / 'NATIVE_FUDAN_REFERENCE_OBJECTIVE_CONTRACT_ACTUAL.json'
    assert not runtime_path.exists()
    runtime_partial = runtime_path.with_suffix('.json.partial')
    with runtime_partial.open('xb') as stream:
        stream.write(runtime_bytes)
    runtime_partial.replace(runtime_path)
    assert bridge.pi_updates == 0 and bridge.wm_updates == 0 and bridge.trace is None
    runtime_order = dict(
        actual_runtime_order_readback=True, complete_named_permutation=True,
        before_first_physical_step_and_Pi=True,
        scope='before first native learning control/Pi; original environment construction/reset is separate',
        actual_robot_joint_names=actual_joint_names,
        reference_artifact_SHA=reference_bundle['reference']['reference_artifact_SHA'],
        runtime_objective_contract_SHA=runtime_contract_SHA,
        source_SHA=task['source_SHA'], original_run_id=go['run_id'],
        actual_own_task_physics_commands_and_success_unchanged=True)
    put(out / 'NATIVE_FUDAN_REFERENCE_RUNTIME_ORDER_ACTUAL.json', runtime_order)
    cfg = SuperMimicConfig(iterations=1000000, buffer_steps=64, utd=1 / 24, horizon=16, goal_frames=1, hist=10, z_dim=32, wm_hidden=(512, 512, 512), policy_hidden=(512, 256, 128), critic_hidden=(256, 128), wm_window=8, wm_batches=10, wm_batch=512, pi_batch=512, warmup_steps=48, wm_only_iters=10, critic_epochs=2, critic_minibatches=2, ckpt_every=1000000, log_every=1, amp='none', compile=False, log_backend='none', seed=20261026)
    # Mandatory closed native source admission; missing85M2 closure stays blocked.
    binding, metadata = read_pinned_metadata_bundle(B / 'NATIVE_CONTINUATION_BINDING_ACTUAL.json')
    admission = admit_closed_source(binding, current_runtime_order=runtime_order,
        current_runtime_objective=runtime_objective, **metadata)
    assert task['source_SHA'] == go['source_SHA'] == admission['checkpoint_SHA'], 'Native source file/task/GO binding differs'
    new_episode_proof = json.loads((Path(log_dir) / 'STEP270_FULLSTATE_RESUME_STARTUP_VERIFIED.json').read_bytes())
    def restore_post_reset_rng(state):
        torch.random.set_rng_state(state[0])
        torch.cuda.set_rng_state_all(state[1])
    learner = construct_preserving_post_reset_global_rng(
        lambda: SuperMimicLearner(bridge, cfg, out / 'native_supermimic', device=env.device, backend='original_Isaac_MOD4_H1_step270'),
        lambda: (torch.random.get_rng_state(), torch.cuda.get_rng_state_all()),
        restore_post_reset_rng)
    bridge.learner = learner
    restore_receipt = restore_legacy85_new_episode(source, learner, bridge, admission,
        new_episode_proof, verify_native)
    put(out / 'NATIVE_CONTINUATION_STARTUP_ACTUAL.json', restore_receipt)
    from native_policy_phase_coverage_v1 import attach_optional_policy_phase_coverage
    bridge.phase_sampling_diagnostic = attach_optional_policy_phase_coverage(learner, out / 'NATIVE_POLICY_PHASE_SAMPLE_COVERAGE_ACTUAL.json')
    oldsave = runner.save

    publisher = NativeCheckpointPublisher(oldsave, learner, bridge, out, producer=dict(
        bridge_source_SHA=hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
        finalization_source_SHA=finalization_source_sha(),
        bootstrap_task_source_SHA=BOOTSTRAP_SHA,
        native_continuation_source_checkpoint_SHA=admission['checkpoint_SHA'],
        native_continuation_source_run_id=admission['source_run_id'],
        continuation_source_SHA=hashlib.sha256((B / 'native_continuation_v1.py').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
        reference_artifact_SHA=reference_bundle['reference']['reference_artifact_SHA'],
        adapter_CPU_report_SHA=reference_bundle['adapter_CPU']['report_SHA'],
        objective_contract_SHA=reference_bundle['adapter_CPU']['objective_contract_SHA'],
        runtime_objective_contract_SHA=runtime_contract_SHA,
        native_not_production_H1_or_E2_source=True))

    def save(path, *args, **kwargs):
        return publisher(path, *args, **kwargs)
    runner.save = save
    runner.alg.act = lambda *args, **kwargs: None
    from h1_production_control_prefix_trace_v1 import H1ControlPrefixTrace
    trace = H1ControlPrefixTrace(runner, out / 'control_prefix_raw')
    bridge.trace = trace
    trace.install()
    original_wm = learner.wm_update
    original_pi = learner.policy_update

    def wm_update():
        result = original_wm()
        assert all((torch.isfinite(x).all() for x in result))
        assert all((torch.isfinite(p).all() for p in learner.wm.parameters()))
        bridge.wm_updates += 1
        bridge.last_wm = [float(x) for x in result]
        return result

    def pi_update():
        result = original_pi()
        assert all((torch.isfinite(x).all() for x in result))
        assert all((torch.isfinite(p).all() for p in learner.pi.parameters()))
        bridge.pi_updates += 1
        bridge.last_pi = [float(x) for x in result]
        return result
    learner.wm_update = wm_update
    learner.policy_update = pi_update
    put(out / 'ACTUAL_H1_NEW_METHOD_STARTUP_CONTRACT.json', dict(epoch=time.time(), source_SHA=task['source_SHA'], source_Adam=task['source_Adamstep'], source_actor_critic_STD17Adam_literal_exact=True, actual_SDK_dt_probe=sdk, actual_envs=env.num_envs, physics_dt=env.physics_dt, control_dt=env.step_dt, actual_geometry=env.h1_phase.state_dict()['geometry'], new_actor128_history10_goal73_not_PPO35_matched_comparison=True, reference_is_partial_retarget_not_our_successful_step_or_forced_action=True, actual_original_goal_machine_unchanged=True, actual_original_trace_sampler_retention_unchanged=True, source_G1_motion_library_not_used=True))
    return run_native_lifecycle(
        train=learner.train, stop_types=(OwnCompleteSavedStop,),
        persist_trace=trace.persist, save=save, out=out,
        iteration=lambda: learner.iteration,
        world_updates=lambda: bridge.wm_updates,
        policy_updates=lambda: bridge.pi_updates,
        events=trace.lifetime_goal_counter.snapshot,
        write_manifest=continuation_manifest_writer(atomic_json, bridge, restore_receipt))

"""Source122: wheel commands plus explicitly qualified jump/cross history.

Engineering coefficients; source-only until full actual target admission.
No action/force/reset controller. Physical original270 acceptance is separate.
"""
import torch
from direct_wheel_height_command_reward_v1 import CommandReward,smooth01,height_score


def evaluate(m,mode,legacy_nonrequest,effort_quality,*,launched,crossed,closure_m,public_age,p=CommandReward()):
    P=mode['top_support'];v=m['world_velocity3'];active=m['request_active']
    landing=P*crossed.to(P.dtype)
    desired_x=mode['base_command_vx']*(1-landing)
    velocity=torch.exp(-((v[...,0]-desired_x).square()+v[...,1].square())/p.velocity_sigma**2)
    heading=(1-m['heading_cos'].clamp(-1,1)).clamp_min(0)
    alignment=torch.exp(-heading/(1-.9800665778412416)-m['root_y'].square()/.3**2)
    # Extra jump commands apply before the leading edge, not to post-edge bouncing.
    leading_front=m['wheel_high2'][...,0].amax(-1)
    before_edge=smooth01((p.edge_x-leading_front)/.04)
    near=mode['near']*before_edge
    height_gate=mode['near']*torch.maximum(before_edge,launched.to(v.dtype))
    forward=(v[...,0]/p.speed).clamp(0,1).square()
    H=height_score(m['wheel_bottom2'],p)
    upward_target=(8*(p.step_height-m['wheel_bottom2'].amin(-1))).clamp(0,2.4)
    # Exact world-Z velocity of each wheel's current lowest mesh support point.
    upward=torch.exp(-((m['wheel_bottom_velocity2']-upward_target[...,None])/.8).square()).amin(-1)
    # A stationary wheel receives zero upward-command bonus, including the
    # small positive Gaussian tail at ground. Both wheels must actually rise.
    upward=upward*(m['wheel_bottom_velocity2'].amin(-1)/2.4).clamp(0,1)
    quiet=torch.exp(-v.square().sum(-1)/.15**2-m['world_angular_velocity3'].square().sum(-1)/.3**2
        -(1-m['upright_cos'].clamp(-1,1)).clamp_min(0)/(1-.984807753012208)
        -heading/(1-.9800665778412416))
    k=4*.01*effort_quality
    quality=(closure_m/.002).clamp(0,1).square()*(public_age>=10).to(v.dtype)
    terms=dict(command_world_velocity=k*velocity*alignment*active,
        qualified_near_both_wheel_height270=k*height_gate*(1-P)*H*forward*alignment*active,
        before_edge_both_wheel_upward_command=k*.5*near*(1-P)*upward*forward*alignment*active,
        supported_top_after_qualified_cross=k*3*landing*active,
        quiet_top_after_qualified_cross=k*landing*quiet*active,
        closure_compliance_cost=torch.zeros_like(k),
        original_nonrequest_recovery=(1-active)*legacy_nonrequest)
    positive=sum(value for name,value in terms.items() if name not in ('closure_compliance_cost','original_nonrequest_recovery'))
    terms['closure_compliance_cost']=-.5*quality*positive
    total=sum(terms.values())
    if not bool(torch.isfinite(total).all()):raise ValueError('nonfinite122 reward')
    return dict(total=total,selected_terms=terms,command_vx=desired_x,
        command_wheel_bottom_height=.27,command_wheel_bottom_upward_velocity=upward_target,
        planning_top_support=P,qualified_cross=crossed,before_edge_gate=before_edge,
        both_wheel_height_score=H,closure_quality_cost=quality,
        source_only_not_runtime_admitted=True,not_original_Fudan_two_policy_reward=True)


def combine_closure_risk(old_soft_done,closure_m,public_age):
    """Continuous engineering constraint-risk cost, not calibrated failure probability.

    The physical2mm guard is unchanged. Predicted1.2..1.8mm now has nonzero
    cost even though the WM underestimates the saved actual failure. This does
    not claim the underlying WM closure prediction has been repaired.
    """
    risk=(closure_m/.002).clamp(0,1).square()
    risk=torch.where(public_age>=10,risk,torch.zeros_like(risk))
    per_step=-torch.expm1(torch.log1p(-risk.clamp_max(1-1e-6))/32.)
    combined=1-(1-old_soft_done)*(1-per_step)
    return torch.where((public_age>=10)&(closure_m>.002),torch.ones_like(combined),combined)

"""Reward process metadata with independent left/right crossing latches.

V1 simultaneous-cross draft is NOTSELECTED and retained. Real270 permits the
two wheels to cross at different callbacks. No geometry/physics tolerance added.
"""
import torch
BASE=1<<20
STRIDE=4096


def unpack(frame):
    base=torch.remainder(frame,BASE);c=torch.div(frame,BASE,rounding_mode='floor')
    crossed2=torch.stack(((c&32)!=0,(c&128)!=0),-1)
    return dict(base=base,public_age=torch.remainder(base,STRIDE),
        delay_ticks=torch.div(base,STRIDE,rounding_mode='floor'),ready=(c&1)!=0,
        air_controls=(c>>1)&7,launched=(c&16)!=0,crossed2=crossed2,
        crossed=crossed2.all(-1)&((c&1)!=0)&((c&16)!=0)&((c&64)!=0),positive_vz=(c&64)!=0)


def code(*,ready,air_controls,launched,crossed2,positive_vz):
    return (ready.long()+2*air_controls.long().clamp(0,4)+16*launched.long()
        +32*crossed2[...,0].long()+128*crossed2[...,1].long()+64*positive_vz.long())


def with_code(frame,process_code):
    return torch.remainder(frame,BASE)+BASE*process_code


def advance(frame,*,clean_air,root_vz,previous_front2,next_front2,wheel_bottom2,edge_x=.35):
    old=unpack(frame);age=old['public_age']+1;request=(age>=200)&(age<2000)
    positive=old['positive_vz']|(request&(root_vz>.05))
    near=((edge_x-next_front2.amin(-1))<=.18)&(next_front2.amax(-1)<edge_x)
    air=request&old['ready']&positive&clean_air&(near|old['launched'])
    ticks=torch.where(air,(old['air_controls']+1).clamp_max(4),0)
    launched=old['launched']|(air&(ticks>=4))
    crossing=(previous_front2<edge_x)&(next_front2>=edge_x)&(wheel_bottom2>=.27)
    crossed=old['crossed2']|(crossing&(launched&clean_air)[...,None])
    c=code(ready=old['ready'],air_controls=ticks,launched=launched,crossed2=crossed,positive_vz=positive)
    return with_code(frame+1,c)


def goal_process_cues(frame):
    r=unpack(frame)
    # Both individual cross flags are visible; no hidden one-wheel history.
    return torch.stack((r['ready'].float(),r['positive_vz'].float(),r['air_controls'].float()/4.,
                        r['launched'].float(),r['crossed2'][...,0].float(),r['crossed2'][...,1].float()),-1)


def inject_goal_cues(goal,frame):
    """Six declared dimensionless process cues, not physical dq targets.

    Previously unknown goal-value slots43..48 and matching masks116..121 are
    typed as process metadata. False cues remain literal old zeros. No network
    widths/parameters/norm buffers are added or reset.
    """
    shaped=goal.reshape(*goal.shape[:-1],10,146).clone()
    c=goal_process_cues(frame).to(goal.dtype)
    shaped[...,43:49]=.005*c.unsqueeze(-2)
    shaped[...,116:122]=(c>0).to(goal.dtype).unsqueeze(-2)
    return shaped.reshape_as(goal)

"""Own authored collision-shape support bounds for planning, not contact truth."""
import torch
from torch import nn


class OwnProcessGeometry(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self,geometry,body_names):
        super().__init__();self.rows=[]
        for i,s in enumerate(geometry['shapes']):
            kind=s['type'];body=body_names.index(s['body'])
            self.register_buffer('L'+str(i),torch.tensor(s['linear_body_from_shape'],dtype=torch.float32))
            self.register_buffer('o'+str(i),torch.tensor(s['origin_body_m'],dtype=torch.float32))
            if kind=='Mesh':
                self.register_buffer('pts'+str(i),torch.tensor(s['points'],dtype=torch.float32))
            elif kind not in ('Cube','Cylinder','Capsule'):
                raise ValueError('Unqualified authored collision shape '+kind)
            self.rows.append((body,kind,dict(s)))
        self.wheel_ids=[body_names.index(x) for x in ('left_wheel','right_wheel')]

    def bounds(self,g):
        lows=[];highs=[]
        for i,(body,kind,s) in enumerate(self.rows):
            R=g['body_rotation'][...,body,:,:];p=g['body_position'][...,body,:]
            A=R@getattr(self,'L'+str(i));centre=p+(R@getattr(self,'o'+str(i))[...,None]).squeeze(-1)
            if kind=='Mesh':
                points=(A@getattr(self,'pts'+str(i)).T).transpose(-1,-2)+centre[...,None,:]
                low=points.amin(-2);high=points.amax(-2)
            else:
                if kind=='Cube':
                    radius=A.abs().sum(-1)*(float(s['size'])/2.)
                else:
                    axis=int(s['axis']);radial=[j for j in range(3) if j!=axis]
                    segment=A[...,axis].abs()*(float(s['height'])/2.)
                    if kind=='Cylinder':
                        radius=segment+float(s['radius'])*torch.sqrt(A[...,radial].square().sum(-1).clamp_min(0))
                    else:
                        radius=segment+float(s['radius'])*torch.sqrt(A.square().sum(-1).clamp_min(0))
                low=centre-radius;high=centre+radius
            lows.append(low);highs.append(high)
        return torch.stack(lows,-2),torch.stack(highs,-2)

    def nonwheel_clearance(self,g):
        low,high=self.bounds(g)
        keep=[i for i,(body,_,_) in enumerate(self.rows) if body not in self.wheel_ids]
        low=low[...,keep,:];high=high[...,keep,:]
        overlap=(high[...,0]>=.35)&(low[...,0]<=1.15)&(high[...,1]>=-.6)&(low[...,1]<=.6)
        surface=torch.where(overlap,.27,0.)
        return (low[...,2]-surface).amin(-1)


def wheel_bottom_velocity2(g,wheel_geometry):
    result=[]
    for i,name in enumerate(('left_wheel','right_wheel')):
        body=wheel_geometry.body_names.index(name)
        R=g['body_rotation'][...,body,:,:]
        points=getattr(wheel_geometry,'points'+str(i))
        offset=(R@points.T).transpose(-1,-2)
        index=offset[...,2].argmin(-1)
        selected=torch.gather(offset,-2,index[...,None,None].expand(*index.shape,1,3)).squeeze(-2)
        velocity=g['body_linear_velocity'][...,body,:]+torch.cross(g['body_angular_velocity'][...,body,:],selected,dim=-1)
        result.append(velocity[...,2])
    return torch.stack(result,-1)

"""Metadata observer on original400Hz phase callback in a NEW source namespace.
Original physical callback runs once and its result/counters remain unchanged.
"""
import types
import torch
from jump_process_frame122_v2 import code


def install(env):
    phase=env.h1_phase
    if getattr(phase,'_reward122_installed',False):raise ValueError('duplicate process observer')
    phase._reward122_air_ticks=torch.zeros(env.num_envs,dtype=torch.long,device=env.device)
    phase._reward122_launched=torch.zeros(env.num_envs,dtype=torch.bool,device=env.device)
    phase._reward122_crossed2=torch.zeros((env.num_envs,2),dtype=torch.bool,device=env.device)
    original=phase.physical
    def physical(this,**kw):
        before=this.crossed.clone()
        result=original(**kw)
        low=this.last_measured['step_wheel_min']-env.scene.env_origins[:,None,:]
        high=this.last_measured['step_wheel_max']-env.scene.env_origins[:,None,:]
        near=((.35-high[...,0].amin(-1))<=.18)&(high[...,0].amax(-1)<.35)
        lane=(low[...,1].amin(-1)>=-.6)&(high[...,1].amax(-1)<=.6)
        clean=result['air']&this.goal.attempt&this.goal.positive_vz&lane&(near|this._reward122_launched)
        this._reward122_air_ticks=torch.where(clean,this._reward122_air_ticks+1,0)
        this._reward122_launched|=clean&(this._reward122_air_ticks>=16)&this.goal.launched
        this._reward122_crossed2|=(this.crossed&~before)&this._reward122_launched[:,None]
        return result
    phase.physical=types.MethodType(physical,phase)
    # Original reset iterates every phase tensor and already resets these fields.
    phase._reward122_installed=True
    return dict(original_callback_single_call=True,no_action_or_force_or_reset_change=True,
        real_clean_air_from_original_allbody_contact_and_geometry=True,real_air_callbacks=16,
        original_crossing_and_goal_counters_unchanged=True)


def current_process_code(env):
    p=env.h1_phase
    if not getattr(p,'_reward122_installed',False):raise ValueError('process observer not installed')
    return code(ready=p.goal.attempt|p.goal.was_ready,
        air_controls=(p._reward122_air_ticks//4).clamp_max(4),
        launched=p._reward122_launched,crossed2=p._reward122_crossed2,positive_vz=p.goal.positive_vz)

===== FULL UTF8 ENTRY C:\Users\lulu\Desktop\claude\mjcf\MOD4_H1_SCUT_STEP270_ON_210_E3_CONTINUATION_20261021\MOD4_H1_FUDAN270_QUALIFIED_JUMP_PROCESS_RESOURCE_V123_20261029\payloads_v2\seed28\source\legacy_fudan111_reward_diagnostics_v1.py SEGMENT 0 =====
"""MOD4 adaptation, not a claim of the upstream controller or a learned skill.

Instantaneous formulas/weights derive from yly-true/fudan_rl_wheel_leg@8204e853.
Upstream original BSD headers and complete source are preserved in pinned_exact_v2.
No force/action override, learner, model, physics, reset, or success gate lives here.
The native127 representation lacks prior-prior action, prior dq and nonwheel
contact forces. Those upstream terms are explicitly omitted, never forged zero.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
import torch

UPSTREAM_COMMIT='8204e853dfd2ed06d85a322e1a998c3d20a3be2c'
NATIVE_UNAVAILABLE_OR_STATEFUL_ORIGINAL_TERMS=(
    'encourage_jump_history_accumulator','collision_nonwheel_contact_force',
    'dof_acc_prior_velocity','action_smooth_prior_prior_action')

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BoundOwnStepReward:
    extension_length_m: float
    tuck_length_m: float
    standing_body_height_m: float
    apex_body_height_m: float
    step_edge_x_m: float
    platform_end_x_m: float
    step_top_z_m: float
    goal_root_x_m: float
    platform_half_width_m: float=.6
    approach_speed_m_s: float=.5
    control_dt_s: float=.01
    def __post_init__(self):
        assert 0<self.tuck_length_m<self.extension_length_m
        assert 0<self.standing_body_height_m<self.apex_body_height_m
        assert self.step_edge_x_m<self.goal_root_x_m<self.platform_end_x_m
        assert self.step_top_z_m>0 and self.control_dt_s==.01 and self.platform_half_width_m==.6

def _weighted(terms,weights,clip,dt):
    assert set(terms)==set(weights)
    weighted={k:(terms[k]*weights[k]).clamp(-clip,clip)*dt for k in terms}
    return torch.stack(tuple(weighted.values()),-1).sum(-1),weighted

def instantaneous_jump_terms(m,p):
    """Original memory-free jump formulas; contact switches supplied per step."""
    flight=m['flight'];takeoff=m['takeoff'];L0=m['virtual_leg_length'];theta=m['virtual_leg_angle'];vz=m['world_vertical_velocity']
    e=(m['task_planar_velocity']-m['task_command_planar_velocity']).square().sum(-1)
    return dict(
        tracking_lin_vel=2*torch.exp(-e/.25),
        tracking_lin_vel_enhance=2*(torch.exp(-e/2.5)-1),
        tracking_ang_vel=torch.exp(-(m['body_yaw_velocity']-m['command_yaw_velocity']).square()/.25),
        flight=flight,
        encourage_jump_upward_only=.15*vz.clamp_min(0),
        base_height_flight=torch.exp(-(m['root_height']-p.apex_body_height_m).abs()*6)*flight,
        leg_tuck=torch.exp(-(L0-p.tuck_length_m).abs().sum(-1)*4)*flight,
        takeoff_extend=torch.exp(-(L0-p.extension_length_m).abs().sum(-1)*4)*takeoff,
        line_z=vz.clamp_min(0)*flight,
        pen_theta_no0=theta.square().sum(-1),
        action_rate=(m['action']-m['previous_action']).square().sum(-1),
        torques=m['actuated_torque'].square().sum(-1),
        orientation=m['projected_gravity'][...,:2].square().sum(-1),
        ang_vel_xy=m['body_angular_velocity'][...,:2].square().sum(-1),
        nominal_state=(theta[...,0]-theta[...,1]).square()+10*(L0[...,0]-L0[...,1]).square())

JUMP_WEIGHTS=dict(tracking_lin_vel=1.,tracking_lin_vel_enhance=1.,tracking_ang_vel=1.,flight=.15,
    encourage_jump_upward_only=1.,base_height_flight=6.,leg_tuck=1.7,takeoff_extend=.5,line_z=6.,
    pen_theta_no0=-2.,action_rate=-.04,torques=-.00005,orientation=-25.,ang_vel_xy=-.1,nominal_state=-1.)

def instantaneous_stair_recovery_terms(m,p):
    e=(m['task_planar_velocity']-m['task_command_planar_velocity']).square().sum(-1)
    # Two authored support planes, never a fictitious interpolated plane.
    zg=(m['root_height']-p.standing_body_height_m).square()
    zt=(m['root_height']-p.standing_body_height_m-p.step_top_z_m).square()
    P=m['top_support']
    height=(1-P)*1.5*torch.exp(-zg*1000)+P*1.5*torch.exp(-zt*1000)
    height_extra=(1-P)*(torch.exp(-zg/.01)-1)+P*(torch.exp(-zt/.01)-1)
    return dict(tracking_lin_vel=torch.exp(-e/.25),tracking_lin_vel_enhance=torch.exp(-e/2.5)-1,
        tracking_ang_vel=torch.exp(-(m['body_yaw_velocity']-m['command_yaw_velocity']).square()/.25),
        base_height=height,base_height_enhance=height_extra,
        nominal_state=(m['virtual_leg_angle'][...,0]-m['virtual_leg_angle'][...,1]).square(),
        lin_vel_z=m['body_vertical_velocity'].square(),ang_vel_xy=m['body_angular_velocity'][...,:2].square().sum(-1),
        orientation=m['projected_gravity'][...,:2].square().sum(-1),dof_vel=m['actuated_leg_dq'].square().sum(-1),
        torques=m['actuated_torque'].square().sum(-1),action_rate=(m['action']-m['previous_action']).square().sum(-1),
        dof_pos_limits=m['actuated_soft_limit_violation'])

RECOVERY_WEIGHTS=dict(tracking_lin_vel=1.5,tracking_lin_vel_enhance=1.5,tracking_ang_vel=1.,base_height=1.,
    base_height_enhance=1.,nominal_state=-1.,lin_vel_z=-.1,ang_vel_xy=-.05,orientation=-10.,dof_vel=-.00005,
    torques=-.0001,action_rate=-.05,dof_pos_limits=-1.)

def contact_and_step_modes(wheel_low,wheel_high,force_z,root_x,root_vz,p):
    """Refresh from each actual/predicted state. These are planning surrogates."""
    positive_force=force_z.clamp_min(0)
    support=positive_force/(positive_force+1.)  # force unit N; exact zero at unloaded history
    flight=(1-support).prod(-1)
    ground=1-flight
    front_gap=p.step_edge_x_m-wheel_high[...,0].amax(-1)
    full_rear_gap=wheel_low[...,0].amin(-1)-p.step_edge_x_m
    far_gap=p.platform_end_x_m-wheel_high[...,0].amax(-1)
    side_low_gap=wheel_low[...,1].amin(-1)+p.platform_half_width_m
    side_high_gap=p.platform_half_width_m-wheel_high[...,1].amax(-1)
    def inside(gap):
        t=(gap/.02).clamp(0,1)
        return t*t*(3-2*t)
    # Exact zero outside any authored XY bound. Differentiable C1 interior ramp.
    footprint=inside(full_rear_gap)*inside(far_gap)*inside(side_low_gap)*inside(side_high_gap)
    top_height=torch.exp(-((wheel_low[...,2]-p.step_top_z_m)/.02).square().mean(-1))
    top_support=footprint*top_height*support.prod(-1)
    # Approach/crossing band lasts to the actual platform far edge.
    near=torch.sigmoid((.18-front_gap)/.03)*torch.sigmoid(far_gap/.03)
    jump=(near+flight).clamp(0,1)*(1-top_support)
    recovery=1-jump
    takeoff=ground*torch.sigmoid((root_vz-.15)/.05)
    return dict(flight=flight,takeoff=takeoff,jump=jump,recovery=recovery,top_support=top_support,
                footprint=footprint,full_rear_gap=full_rear_gap,side_low_gap=side_low_gap,side_high_gap=side_high_gap,
                command_forward_velocity=p.approach_speed_m_s*(1-top_support),
                support_surface_height=p.step_top_z_m*top_support)  # diagnostic expectation only; never used as a reward plane

def combined_reward(m,p,modes):
    """Native110 common baseline plus mode advantages; declared MOD4 adaptation."""
    j,jparts=_weighted(instantaneous_jump_terms(m,p),JUMP_WEIGHTS,2.5,p.control_dt_s)
    r,rparts=_weighted(instantaneous_stair_recovery_terms(m,p),RECOVERY_WEIGHTS,1.,p.control_dt_s)
    # Physical success still uses its exact contact/footprint/12+200 counters.
    # These smooth planning cues now include static heading and tilt, not only motion.
    tilt_error=(1-m['upright_cos']).clamp_min(0)
    heading_error=(1-m['heading_cos']).clamp_min(0)
    quiet=torch.exp(-m['root_planar_velocity'].square().sum(-1)/.03**2
        -m['world_vertical_velocity'].square()/.03**2
        -m['body_angular_velocity'].square().sum(-1)/.3**2
        -tilt_error/(1-0.984807753012208)
        -heading_error/(1-0.9800665778412416))
    # Dense task cues share the recovery baseline; neither is mode-blended.
    task_alignment=.5*p.control_dt_s*(torch.exp(-heading_error/(1-0.9800665778412416))
        +torch.exp(-m['root_lateral_position'].square()/.3**2))*m['request_active']
    placement=modes['top_support']*quiet
    # All six bonuses are nonnegative. The recovery-specific residual is
    # nonpositive: base_height is capped at dt, other two terms are costs.
    jump_bonus=sum(jparts[k] for k in
        ('flight','encourage_jump_upward_only','base_height_flight','leg_tuck','takeoff_extend','line_z'))
    recovery_extra=rparts['base_height']+rparts['base_height_enhance']+rparts['lin_vel_z']-p.control_dt_s
    mode_advantage=jump_bonus-recovery_extra
    # Equivalent to common + jump*bonus + recovery*extra + dt.
    # At jump=0 this is exactly the original recovery reward (no new alive offset).
    total=r+modes['jump']*mode_advantage+12.*p.control_dt_s*placement+task_alignment
    assert torch.isfinite(total).all()
    return dict(total=total,jump_reward=j,recovery_reward=r,jump_terms=jparts,recovery_terms=rparts,
                own_top_support_quiet_placement=placement,unsupported_original_terms=NATIVE_UNAVAILABLE_OR_STATEFUL_ORIGINAL_TERMS,
                actual_success_not_inferred=True,nonnegative_jump_bonus=jump_bonus,
                nonpositive_recovery_extra=recovery_extra,mode_advantage=mode_advantage,task_alignment=task_alignment,top_quiet=quiet)

"""Candidate116 reward API. Legacy equations are retained for diagnostics/off-request.
Every training term is explicitly present in selected_optimization_terms.
"""
import torch
from legacy_fudan111_reward_diagnostics_v1 import (
    BoundOwnStepReward,UPSTREAM_COMMIT,NATIVE_UNAVAILABLE_OR_STATEFUL_ORIGINAL_TERMS,
    _weighted,instantaneous_jump_terms,instantaneous_stair_recovery_terms,JUMP_WEIGHTS,RECOVERY_WEIGHTS,
    contact_and_step_modes as legacy_modes)
from direct_wheel_height_command_reward_v1 import CommandReward,modes as command_modes
from jump_process_command_reward122_v1 import evaluate
PARAMETERS=CommandReward()

def contact_and_step_modes(low,high,force_z,root_x,root_vz,p):
    assert p.step_top_z_m==PARAMETERS.step_height and p.step_edge_x_m==PARAMETERS.edge_x and p.platform_end_x_m==PARAMETERS.far_x
    old=legacy_modes(low,high,force_z,root_x,root_vz,p)
    new=command_modes(low,high,force_z,root_x,PARAMETERS)
    old.update(new,top_support=new['top_support'],jump=new['near']*(1-new['top_support']),recovery=1-new['near']*(1-new['top_support']),
        command_forward_velocity=new['base_command_vx']*(1-new['top_support']),wheel_bottom2=low[...,2],
        command_reward116=True,legacy_flight_is_diagnostic_only=True)
    return old

def combined_reward(m,p,mode):
    j,jparts=_weighted(instantaneous_jump_terms(m,p),JUMP_WEIGHTS,2.5,p.control_dt_s)
    r,rparts=_weighted(instantaneous_stair_recovery_terms(m,p),RECOVERY_WEIGHTS,1.,p.control_dt_s)
    # Preserve original penalty magnitudes only as a small bounded attenuation;
    # they cannot turn all task rewards negative and incentivize early falling.
    effort_names=('action_rate','torques','dof_pos_limits','nominal_state','orientation','ang_vel_xy','dof_vel')
    penalty=sum(rparts[k] for k in effort_names)/p.control_dt_s
    assert bool((penalty<=1e-6).all())
    effort=torch.exp(.05*penalty.clamp_min(-10.))
    data=dict(wheel_bottom2=mode['wheel_bottom2'],world_velocity3=torch.cat((m['task_planar_velocity'],m['world_vertical_velocity'][...,None]),-1),
        world_angular_velocity3=m['world_angular_velocity'],request_active=m['request_active'],heading_cos=m['heading_cos'],upright_cos=m['upright_cos'],root_y=m['root_lateral_position'])
    data.update(wheel_high2=m['wheel_high2'],wheel_bottom_velocity2=m['wheel_bottom_velocity2'])
    out=evaluate(data,mode,r,effort,launched=m['process_launched'],crossed=m['process_crossed'],closure_m=m['closure_m'],public_age=m['public_age'],p=PARAMETERS)
    assert bool(((out['total']>=-1e-7)|(m['request_active']==0)).all())
    return dict(total=out['total'],selected_optimization_terms=out['selected_terms'],
        process_command122=out,command_tracking116=dict(command_vx=out['command_vx'],target_each_wheel_bottom_m=PARAMETERS.step_height,
            actual_wheel_bottom2=mode['wheel_bottom2'],both_wheel_height_score=out['both_wheel_height_score'],
            forward_command_fraction_squared=(m['task_planar_velocity'][...,0]/.5).clamp(0,1).square(),near_both_wheels=mode['near'],effort_quality=effort,
            original_leg_length_flight_height_bonuses_not_selected=True,original_flight_is_not_airborne_truth=True),
        jump_reward=j,recovery_reward=r,jump_terms=jparts,recovery_terms=rparts,
        legacy_jump_reward_is_diagnostic_not_optimization=True,actual_success_not_inferred=True,
        own_top_support_quiet_placement=mode['top_support']*m['process_crossed']*torch.exp(-m['world_vertical_velocity'].square()/.15**2),top_quiet=out['selected_terms']['quiet_top_after_qualified_cross'],
        contact_step_modes=mode,unsupported_original_terms=NATIVE_UNAVAILABLE_OR_STATEFUL_ORIGINAL_TERMS)
